### [101] [XENONnT $pp$-Dominated Solar Neutrinos Constrain the $ν_1$ Pseudo-Dirac Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38233)
*Gio Leone*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: XENONnT实验通过分析低能太阳中微子信号（主要为pp中微子），对第一质量本征态的pseudo-Dirac振荡进行研究，限制了pseudo-Dirac质量平方差δm₁² ≳ 5.9×10⁻¹³ eV²（2σ）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用XENONnT对低能太阳中微子的高灵敏度探测能力，在太阳—地球基线上检验超小pseudo-Dirac质量分裂引起的活性—惰性中微子振荡

Method: 建模第一质量本征态的pseudo-Dirac振荡，计算其对pp和⁷Be电子反冲能谱的修正，并基于XENONnT公布的能谱数据构建似然函数

Result: 最佳拟合对应零pseudo-Dirac分裂；在2σ置信水平下排除δm₁² ≳ 5.9×10⁻¹³ eV²

Conclusion: XENONnT首次在该质量尺度上对pseudo-Dirac中微子模型给出强约束，逼近未来pp中微子探测器曾讨论的灵敏区域

Abstract: XENONnT has measured a low-energy solar-neutrino signal dominated by $pp$ neutrinos. At these energies, an ultra-small pseudo-Dirac mass-squared splitting induces detectable active-sterile oscillations over the Sun--Earth baseline. We study pseudo-Dirac oscillations of the first mass eigenstate, compute the resulting modification of the $pp+{}^7$Be electron-recoil spectrum, and construct a likelihood from the published XENONnT energy spectra. We find that the best fit occurs at zero pseudo-Dirac splitting and rule out values of $δm_1^2 \gtrsim 5.9\times10^{-13}\,\mathrm{eV}^2$ at $2 σ$, approaching a regime previously discussed for future $pp$-sensitive detectors.

</details>


### [102] [Searching for BSM Experimental Signatures with Large Lagrangian Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38309)
*Ibrahim Elsharkawy,Victoria Knapp-Perez,Wahid Bhimji,Aishik Ghosh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出hAIthem框架，结合强化学习（RL）与大语言模型（LLM），自动探索超越标准模型（BSM）理论中未被实验排除的参数空间区域，并生成可检验的新物理信号，尤其在暗物质模型中展现出比进化算法更优的搜索能力与物理多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSM物理理论丰富但缺乏足够区分性的实验观测；以暗物质为例，引力证据无法有效约束庞大理论空间，亟需新方法挖掘潜在可观测信号并评估未来实验价值。

Method: 构建基于自回归Transformer的Large Lagrangian Model（LLaM），预训练于约10,000个拉格朗日量（~10亿token），再通过强化学习代理在Battleship式游戏中对抗现象学工具，定位未被排除的高维参数区域；随后用决策树按可观测量划分区域，并由多个LLM代理竞争生成现实可行的物理信号。

Result: 在单暗标量多重态模型子空间中，hAIthem优于进化算法基线，发现更多且物理多样性更高的可行参数区域，并提出新颖可观测量组合（如将无晕依赖运动学比应用于古探测器）。

Conclusion: hAIthem为系统性、自动化探索BSM模型的可观测后果提供了可行范式，凸显RL与LLM协同在理论-实验桥梁构建中的潜力。

Abstract: The search for physics Beyond the Standard Model (BSM) is generally limited not by the supply of theory descriptions but by the lack of discriminating experimental observations. A case in point is dark matter, where the overwhelming gravitational evidence only goes so far in distinguishing between models within a vast theory space. Exploring the space of testable model signatures may help identify overlooked experimental observables and indicate the utility of future experiments. A challenge is designing a search through model signatures outside what is found in the literature. Our primary contribution is hAIthem, a framework that combines the self-guided exploration of reinforcement learning (RL) with the broad literature-derived knowledge of LLMs. We build an RL agent that learns to find which portions of a theory's high-dimensional parameter space are not excluded under some subset of constraints by playing a Battleship-style "game" against a suite of phenomenology tools. The agent is built as a Large Lagrangian Model (LLaM), an autoregressive transformer that reads a tokenized Lagrangian, is pretrained at scale (here on ~1 billion tokens from ~10,000 Lagrangians), and is fine-tuned in a live environment. The framework then constructs a decision tree that separates RL-found regions using observables computed with established tools, and passes the remaining degenerate regions to a set of LLM agents that compete to produce realistic signatures. In this proof of concept, RL-search outperforms an evolutionary-algorithm baseline, finding more viable regions with greater physical diversity. In a restricted space of single dark scalar multiplet models, we find that hAIthem proposes interesting combinations of previously studied observables, such as the application of a halo-independent kinematic ratio to paleo-detectors.

</details>


### [103] [How collective modes terminate in the dispersion relation of neutrino plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38314)
*Damiano F. G. Fiorillo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了中微子等离子体中由于中微子非零质量导致的味波色散关系奇异行为的变化，分类讨论了简并点和模式突然终止两类奇异行为，并推导了其局域展开形式；同时指出非零质量会消除轴对称情形下的简并，而模式突然终止则普遍存在；此外，发现洛伦兹型频率分布的无碰撞等离子体等价于具有有效碰撞率的单色情形，为简化一般分布下的色散关系提供了新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中微子非零质量如何改变味波色散关系中的奇异性（如Landau阻尼模式在光速附近突变消失），并厘清这些奇异性在更现实的有质量中微子情形下的普适性与分类特征。

Method: 理论分析与数学推导：对中微子味等离子体的色散关系进行系统分类，区分退化点（两模式合并）和模式突然终止（频率趋近介电函数分支割线）两类奇异性；推导各自在波矢空间中的局域展开；分析轴对称与三维情形下简并结构的变化；引入洛伦兹型频率分布模型，建立其与有效碰撞率单色模型的严格等价性。

Result: 1) 非零质量消除沿对称轴的简并，但在三维k空间中仍可能存在不经过轴的简并线；2) 模式突然终止是普遍现象，仅当能量分布为整函数时例外；3) 洛伦兹型频率分布的无碰撞系统严格等价于带有效碰撞率的单色系统；4) 任意频率分布可用洛伦兹和近似，大幅简化色散关系求解。

Conclusion: 中微子质量显著改变味波奇异性结构：简并不再普适，而模式突然终止更具鲁棒性；洛伦兹分布等价性提供了一种高效处理实际中微子能谱色散问题的新范式。

Abstract: Neutrino plasmas support flavor waves, collective oscillations of flavor, that neutrinos can resonantly emit or absorb when they fulfill the Cherenkov condition. For massless neutrinos, this condition leads to singular features in the dispersion relation: Landau-damped modes, for example, disappear abruptly as their phase velocity approaches the speed of light. We investigate how these singularities change when nonzero neutrino masses introduce a spread in velocities. We classify singular behavior of the frequency as a function of wavevector into i) degeneracy points, where two modes merge, and ii) abrupt mode termination, when the frequency approaches a branch cut of the flavor dielectric function. For each class, we derive a local expansion and determine where such behavior can occur. Degeneracy points, frequently encountered in axially symmetric dispersion relations in the fast limit, are not generic. Nonzero neutrino masses lift the degeneracy along the symmetry axis; in three-dimensional wavevector space, there might still be degeneracy lines, not necessarily intersecting the symmetry axis. Abrupt mode termination, instead, persists generically, except for special energy distributions which are entire functions. As a byproduct of this study, we show that a collisionless plasma with a Lorentzian neutrino-frequency distribution is exactly equivalent to a monochromatic one with an effective collision rate. Approximating a general frequency distribution by a sum of Lorentzians therefore substantially simplifies the dispersion relation.

</details>


### [104] [Constraints on Complex Electroweak WIMPs from Solar Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38316)
*Juan P. Luengas,Maxim Pospelov,Harikrishnan Ramani*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于电弱多重态的标量和费米子暗物质在太阳中的捕获、能量损失和湮灭过程，推广了先前对希格斯微子的研究，并发现高维多重态表现出自相似行为；IceCube实验结果限制了质量分裂δ≥350 keV，从而排除了大部分电弱暗物质模型作为LZ实验单次高反冲事件的可能来源，而暗物质速度分布的高能尾（如大麦哲伦云引起）会使LZ事件偏好的分裂值超过太阳约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电弱多重态模型是粒子暗物质最有说服力的实现之一，其最轻中性组分可作为稳定Majorana费米子或实标量，其唯象学由暗物质质量mχ和质量分裂δ决定；亚MeV范围的δ对增大非弹性散射截面至关重要，需系统分析其太阳捕获、能量损失与湮灭行为。

Method: 本文推广了先前针对Higgsino的分析，系统研究了标量和费米子电弱多重态暗物质在太阳中的捕获率、能量损失及湮灭过程，并结合IceCube中微子观测结果、热冻结机制及标准晕模型速度分布进行参数约束；同时考虑了大麦哲伦云引起的暗物质高速尾效应对LZ事件解释的影响。

Result: 发现双态情况较特殊，而更高维多重态呈现自相似行为；IceCube结果将δ下限约束为≈350 keV（适用于mχ ≤ 100 TeV），该约束排除了大多数电弱暗物质模型对LZ单次高反冲事件的解释；引入高速尾后，LZ事件所偏好的δ值普遍超过太阳约束。

Conclusion: 电弱多重态暗物质模型在当前中微子与直接探测联合约束下，难以解释LZ实验的单次高反冲事件；除非存在显著偏离标准晕模型的速度分布（如大麦哲伦云贡献的高速尾），否则这类模型作为暗物质候选者的可行性受到严重挑战。

Abstract: One of the most convincing realizations of particle dark matter is provided by models based on electroweak multiplets. The lightest neutral state of such a multiplet can be a stable Majorana fermion or a real scalar, and its phenomenology is governed by two parameters: the dark matter mass and the mass splitting $\{m_χ,δ\}$. The sub-MeV range for $δ$ is particularly important as it allows for a large inelastic scattering cross section. In this paper, we analyze solar capture, energy loss, and annihilation of scalar and fermionic electroweak multiplet dark matter, generalizing earlier results for the Higgsino to other representations. We find that, while the case of a doublet is somewhat special, higher representations behave in a self-similar way. For the higher multiplets, IceCube results constrain the splitting to $δ\gtrsim 350$ keV for all dark matter masses up to $100$ TeV. Assuming thermal freeze-out and a Standard Halo Model velocity distribution, this rules out most models of electroweak dark matter as a possible origin of the single high-recoil event reported by the LZ collaboration. We also show that a high-velocity tail in the dark matter distribution, such as that induced by the Large Magellanic Cloud, raises the splitting preferred by the LZ event above the solar bounds for many of the multiplets.

</details>


### [105] [A Partial Wave Formalism for High-Energy Meson Electroproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38337)
*Dillon Leahy,Derek Glazier,Vincent Mathieu,Marco Filippini,David Ireland,Glòria Montaña,Alessandro Pilloni,Bhawani Singh,Daniel Winney*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反射振幅的介子电产生过程的全面分波分析形式论，利用宇称对称性构建了完全对角化的框架，实现了纵向与横向截面的本征分离，并通过实验数据验证了其有效性，为未来强子谱学和核子结构研究提供了统一工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种不依赖模型假设、能直接从实验观测中提取完整振幅的形式论，以克服传统Rosenbluth分离等方法的局限性，并支持高精度强子物理研究。

Method: 基于反射振幅构建满足宇称对称性的全对角化分波分析框架；结合高能Regge因子化假设实现横纵向截面本征分离；通过数值闭合检验及HERMES/COMPASS的ρ⁰和ω电产生数据进行验证；推广至含靶/反冲重子极化的通用情形。

Result: 成功从实验数据中无模型依赖地提取出ρ⁰和ω电产生振幅：ρ⁰结果符合s-道螺旋度守恒预期，ω结果清晰分离出大的非自然宇称π交换贡献；建立了适用于极化实验的完备可观测量体系。

Conclusion: 该形式论提供了一个统一、严格且可推广的分波分析框架，无需因子化假设即可完成振幅完全提取，可直接服务于CLAS12、GlueX及未来电子-离子对撞机（EIC）的关键物理目标。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive partial-wave analysis formalism for meson electroproduction based on reflectivity amplitudes. By applying parity-based symmetries, we construct a fully diagonalized framework that relates complex production amplitudes to measurable spherical harmonic moments and Spin Density Matrix Elements. We demonstrate that, under the assumption of high-energy Regge factorization, longitudinal and transverse cross sections can be natively separated without requiring beam-energy-varying Rosenbluth separations. We validate this inversion methodology through numerical closure tests and apply it to existing unpolarized $ρ^0$ and $ω$ electroproduction data from HERMES and COMPASS. The extracted amplitudes align with $s$-channel helicity conservation expectations for the $ρ^0$, while clearly isolating the large unnatural-parity pion exchange contribution in $ω$ production without model-dependent background approximations. Finally, we systematically generalize the formalism to include initial target and recoil baryon polarization, establishing the rigorous observables required for complete amplitude extraction, no longer requiring any factorisation assumptions. This unified framework directly supports upcoming spectroscopy and nucleon structure programs at CLAS12, GlueX, and the future Electron-Ion Collider.

</details>


### [106] [Imprints of Left-Right Symmetry breaking in Gravitational Wave Spectroscopy: A non-minimal case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38431)
*Abdul Rahaman Shaikh,Nandini Das,Rathin Adhikari,Anindya Datta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非最小左-右对称规范模型中的宇宙相变动力学，发现该模型在高能标下可产生强一阶相变，并产生可在LISA等引力波探测器中观测到的随机引力波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索左-右对称模型中由丰富标量扇区驱动的一阶相变及其可能产生的可观测引力波信号，以连接粒子物理与早期宇宙学。

Method: 基于从E6大统一群导出的非最小左-右对称模型（SU(2)_L × SU(2)_R × U(1)_R × U(1)_L），在满足理论与实验约束下，分析SU(2)_R × U(1)_R × U(1)_L → U(1)_Y对称性破缺过程中的有限温有效势，搜寻可产生强一阶相变的参数空间，并计算相应引力波谱。

Result: 在数十TeV及以上能标处发现大范围参数空间支持强一阶相变，并预测其产生的随机引力波信号落在LISA、DECIGO、AEDGE和μARES等当前及下一代引力波探测器的灵敏度范围内。

Conclusion: 该模型不仅自洽且可检验，还为引力波天文学提供了明确的新物理探针，有望通过未来引力波观测验证左-右对称性破缺机制。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of cosmological phase transition in the framework of a non-minimal version of Left-Right symmetric gauge model. At a high energy scale, this framework having the symmetry $SU(2)_L \otimes SU(2)_{R} \otimes U(1)_R \otimes U(1)_L$, is stemmed out from the Grand Unifying group $E_6$. Rich scalar sector of this model offers interesting opportunity for studying first order phase transition which can be the source of stochastic gravitational waves. Taking into account the theoretical and experimental constraints applicable to this model, we look for benchmark parameters where first order phase transition could be observed during the breaking of $SU(2)_{R} \otimes U(1)_R \otimes U(1)_L$ down to $U(1)_Y$, at an energy scale of tens of TeVs or higher. Our investigation reveals the possibility of strong first order phase transition over a large region of parameter space. We also discuss the production of stochastic gravitational waves arising from such strong first order phase transition. Our results present clear, testable predictions that fall directly within the sensitivity bands of ongoing and next-generation gravitational-wave observatories like LISA, DECIGO, AEDGE and $μ$ARES.

</details>


### [107] [QCD corrections to light-quark dipole sensitivity via transverse spin asymmetries at the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38535)
*Sally Dawson,Zhite Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来电子-离子对撞机（EIC）上深度非弹性散射中横向自旋不对称性（TSAs）的下阶量子色动力学（NLO QCD）修正，发现其对新物理具有互补探测能力，主要不确定性来自下夸克横动量部分子分布函数（transversity PDF）的现有精度不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索EIC上利用横向自旋不对称性探测新物理的潜力，并评估NLO QCD修正对其稳健性与灵敏度的影响，以补充LHC Drell-Yan和中子电偶极矩实验的限制。

Method: 理论计算NLO QCD修正对依赖轻夸克偶极算符的横向自旋不对称性的影响，结合宇称、时间反演和手征对称性约束其解析形式。

Result: TSAs在NLO QCD修正下保持稳健，对新物理敏感，且其解析结构受基本对称性严格约束；主要理论不确定性源于下夸克transversity PDF的当前知识不足。

Conclusion: EIC上的TSAs是探测新物理的重要互补探针，需提升transversity PDF（尤其是下夸克）的确定精度以释放其全部潜力。

Abstract: We consider the effects of next-to-leading order (NLO) QCD corrections to transverse spin asymmetries (TSAs) in deep inelastic scattering at the future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) with polarized electron and proton beams that depend on light quark dipole operators. The TSAs are robust under the inclusion of the NLO QCD effects and provide sensitivity to new physics that is complementary to limits extracted from the Drell-Yan measurements at the LHC and from neutron electric dipole moments. The major uncertainty on our results is from the poor existing determination of the transversity parton distribution function for the down quark. Interesting relations from parity and time reversal invariance, along with chiral symmetry, constrain the analytic form of the TSAs.

</details>


### [108] [Inclusive diffraction in eA at small x with the Sartre event generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38963)
*Tobias Toll,Bhakta Naik,Jaswant Singh,Thomas Ullrich*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Sartre事件生成器，实现了小x区域质子和核靶上的包容性衍射深度非弹性散射（DIS），采用色偶极子图像，并结合IPsat和IPnonsat偶极振幅，包含qar{q}横纵模及主导大M_X的qar{q}g组分，最终通过PYTHIA强子化生成完整末态；与H1/ZEUS实验数据及D*衍射产额比较，并给出电子-离子对撞机（EIC）预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为填补小x区域包容性衍射DIS在质子与核靶上缺乏统一、全微分事件生成器的空白，特别是支持未来EIC实验研究。

Method: 基于色偶极子图像，在Sartre框架中引入IPsat和IPnonsat偶极振幅，纳入q\bar{q}（横、纵向）与q\bar{q}g Fock态贡献，并用PYTHIA进行强子化以生成完全排他的强子末态。

Result: 成功实现适用于ep和eA碰撞的小x包容性衍射DIS模拟；与H1/ZEUS包容性衍射数据及D*衍射测量一致；给出EIC可观测的预测；成为目前唯一支持该物理过程的事件生成器。

Conclusion: 所扩展的Sartre生成器显著提升了小x衍射DIS理论建模与实验模拟能力，为EIC时代核子与核结构的衍射探针研究提供了关键工具。

Abstract: We present an extension of the Sar\emph{t}re event generator that implements inclusive diffractive deep inelastic scattering for both proton and nuclear targets at small $x$. The calculation is performed in the colour-dipole picture using the IPsat and IPnonsat dipole amplitudes that Sar\emph{t}re also employs for exclusive processes, and includes the transverse and longitudinal $q\bar q$ contributions together with the $q\bar qg$ Fock state that dominates at large $M_X$. The partonic diffractive system is hadronised with \pythia, giving fully exclusive hadronic final states. We compare to the combined H1 and ZEUS inclusive diffractive measurements and to diffractive $D^*$ production, and present predictions for the Electron-Ion Collider. This makes Sar\emph{t}re the only event generator providing inclusive diffractive DIS in small-$x$ for both $ep$ and $eA$ collisions.

</details>


### [109] [Resummation of threshold double logarithms in gluon fragmentation into $^1S_0^{[1,8]}$ quarkonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39029)
*Hee Sok Chung*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了胶子碎裂为$^1S_0^{[1,8]}$夸克偶素的碎裂函数在阈值附近的双重对数行为，该行为在Mellin空间中被$1/N$压低；作者发展了一套在主导双重对数水平上对此类通道进行阈值双重对数重求和的形式理论，并讨论其唯象学影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知对主导$1/N$幂次的胶子碎裂函数可进行阈值双重对数重求和，但对被$1/N$幂次压低的通道（如$^1S_0^{[1,8]}$）尚无此方法；而这些通道的树图碎裂函数在运动学阈值处非零有限，使得阈值重求和具有重要性。

Method: 推导适用于$1/N$压低通道的阈值双重对数重求和形式理论，在主导双重对数水平上实现重求和。

Result: 建立了针对$^1S_0^{[1,8]}$通道的阈值双重对数重求和形式理论，并评估了其唯象学影响。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了夸克偶素产生中阈值重求和理论的空白，为精确预言$^1S_0^{[1,8]}$通道的碎裂过程提供了新工具。

Abstract: I investigate the threshold double logarithmic behavior of the fragmentation function of a gluon into $^1S_0^{[1,8]}$ quarkonia, which is suppressed by $1/N$ in Mellin space. While threshold double logarithms have been resummed to all orders in the strong coupling for gluon fragmentation functions for quarkonia at leading power in $1/N$, it has not been known how to resum them for channels suppressed by powers of $1/N$. Threshold resummation can be important for the $^1S_0^{[1,8]}$ channels, as the tree-level gluon fragmentation functions have finite yet nonzero values at the kinematical threshold. I derive a formalism for resumming the threshold double logarithms for these channels at the leading double logarithmic level and discuss the phenomenological impact.

</details>


### [110] [Heavy Dark Baryons at Large $N$: Self-Interactions across All Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39158)
*Giovani Dalla Valle Garcia,Juan Herrero-García*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大色数SU(N)禁闭暗区中重暗重子的自相互作用暗物质模型，通过其色电极化率诱导的范德华力，可自然解释从矮星系到星系团不同天体尺度观测到的暗物质自相互作用截面，并可由观测反推色数N，且偏好GeV量级暗重子质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释暗物质在不同天体尺度（如矮星系、星系团、强引力透镜系统）中表现出的宽范围自相互作用截面（σ/m），现有模型难以统一拟合。

Method: 构建一个具有中等大色数N（~10–100）的SU(N)禁闭暗强相互作用理论，其中重暗夸克构成的暗重子因色电极化率产生长程范德华型自相互作用；通过速度依赖的截面行为与多尺度天文观测对比，并利用JVAS B1938+666等系统的动力学约束反推N。

Result: 该模型能自然再现σ/m ≈ 30–100 cm²/g（矮星系）、降至星系团所需值、并在几km/s速度下升至100–1000 cm²/g（吻合B1938+666第二扰动体暗示）；对给定m_Q/Λ_d，可观测数据可约束N；最优质量尺度为GeV量级，且暗重子不对称性可独立解释暗物质丰度。

Conclusion: GeV尺度的重暗重子在中等大N禁闭暗区中，凭借色电极化主导的范德华力，提供了一种自洽、可检验、且无需微调的宽速度范围自相互作用暗物质框架，并将粒子物理参数N与天文观测直接关联。

Abstract: We show that heavy dark baryons in a confining $SU(N)$ sector with a moderately large number of colors, $N\sim10$--$100$, can generate self-interactions spanning the full range of astrophysical velocities. Their chromo-electric polarizability induces van der Waals interactions that yield $σ/m_{\rm DM}\sim30$-$100~\mathrm{cm^2/g}$ at dwarf-galaxy velocities, decrease to the values required by galaxy clusters, and rise to $100$-$1000~\mathrm{cm^2/g}$ at velocities of a few $\mathrm{km/s}$, as suggested by the second perturber of JVAS B1938+666. Remarkably, for a given ratio $m_Q/Λ_d$, these observations can be used to infer the value of $N$. The resulting self-interaction phenomenology favors GeV-scale dark baryons, independently of the relic-abundance requirement. At these masses, a dark baryon asymmetry comparable to the visible one can account for the observed dark-matter abundance.

</details>


### [111] [Heavy Dark Baryons as Self-Interacting Dark Matter: A GeV-Scale Coincidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39193)
*Giovani Dalla Valle Garcia,Juan Herrero-García*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由重矢量类夸克构成的暗SU(N)规范理论中的自相互作用暗物质，发现当颜色数N足够大时，暗重子间的吸引力可与短程泡利排斥力竞争，从而产生强速度依赖的散射截面，适用于解释矮星系和星系团观测，并自然支持不对称暗物质起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释矮星系和星系团中观测到的暗物质自相互作用现象，并探索其与不对称暗物质起源的联系。

Method: 基于暗SU(N)规范理论，利用类夸克偶素算符乘积展开（OPE）方法推导重暗重子间的多极相互作用，包含伦敦色散、卡西米尔-波尔德和暗胶球交换的Yukawa项。

Result: 当N=2,3时吸引力太弱；当N足够大（如SU(10)）时，吸引力可与泡利排斥竞争，得到符合矮星系与星系团约束的速度依赖截面，并在低速下允许更大截面以满足引力热演化需求；对应GeV量级暗重子与接近QCD能标的暗禁闭尺度。

Conclusion: 大N暗SU(N)规范模型可自然实现强速度依赖的暗物质自相互作用，同时兼容多种天体物理观测并暗示不对称暗物质起源。

Abstract: We study self-interacting dark matter composed of heavy baryons in a confining dark $SU(N)$ gauge theory with a single heavy vector-like quark, $m_Q \gg Λ_d$. In this regime, the dark baryons are compact Coulombic bound states whose long-distance interactions can be described by a multipole expansion. We derive the leading baryon-baryon interaction by adapting the quarkonium operator-product-expansion formalism to heavy baryons. The resulting force contains non-retarded London dispersion, retarded Casimir-Polder, and Yukawa contributions from dark glueball exchange. We find that the self-interaction phenomenology depends strongly on the number of colors. For $N=2,3$, the attractive interaction is too weak to overcome the short-distance Pauli repulsion, and no phenomenologically relevant velocity dependence is obtained within our minimal set-up. For sufficiently large $N$, the attractive interaction remains competitive with the repulsive core and can generate strong velocity dependent cross sections. Taking $SU(10)$ as a representative finite-$N$ realization, we find regions compatible with self-interaction requirements inferred from dwarf galaxies and clusters, while allowing much larger cross sections at low velocities relevant for gravothermal evolution. These regions correspond to GeV-scale dark baryons and a confinement scale close to that of QCD, which naturally motivate an asymmetric dark matter origin.

</details>


### [112] [New Observables for Resonant and Non-Resonant New Physics Searches at Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39381)
*Julien Touchèque*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了超越标准模型（BSM）物理的两种通用探测途径：一是利用标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）研究重新粒子的离壳效应，特别通过U(1)味对称性对CP破坏算符的Wilson系数排序，并指出三重积不对称性比截面等常用观测量更敏感；二是针对SU(3)单态与八重态标量，在四顶夸克末态中开展其共振态的在壳搜寻，采用NLO QCD生成、改进的顶夸克喷注识别与背景模拟，给出质量与顶耦合的新限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型虽经Higgs发现而确立，但仍存在诸多未解问题，BSM模型数量庞大难以逐一检验；因此需借助通用模型（如SMEFT及具体新共振态）作为中介，同时探针多种紫外完备理论。

Method: 1）在SMEFT框架下，利用U(1)全局味对称性对Warsaw基中CP-奇算符的Wilson系数进行排序，并构造并分析简单三重积不对称性以提升灵敏度；2）对SU(3)单态与八重态标量在四顶过程中的在壳信号建模，包括标量-双顶关联产生及八重态标量对产生产生强 boosted 顶夸克对，全部过程在NLO QCD精度下生成，并辅以改进的顶喷注识别、背景模拟及新型事件选择技术。

Result: 1）三重积不对称性被证明比微分截面等常规观测量对SMEFT CP破坏算符更敏感、更高效；2）基于LHC四顶数据，对单态与八重态标量的质量及与顶夸克的耦合强度给出了新的实验约束。

Conclusion: 通用模型方法（SMEFT离壳分析 + 特定共振态在壳搜寻）是当前BSM探索的有效策略；三重积观测量可成为未来高精度BSM探测的关键工具；四顶末态结合先进喷注识别与理论建模，显著提升了对新型色荷标量的探测能力。

Abstract: The discovery of the Higgs boson cemented the Standard Model of particle physics (SM) as we know it and observations have so far confirmed SM predictions. Now, too many beyond-the-Standard-Model (BSM) models can be explored to overcome the remaining shortcomings of the SM. As a result, generic models present a useful intermediary step to point towards the next extension of the SM probing multiple Ultraviolet models simultaneously. In this work, two examples are investigated. First, off-shell effects of potential heavy new particles are provided in the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) through higher dimensional operators. In particular, Charge-parity (CP) symmetry violating contributions from Wilson coefficients are ranked by using U(1) global flavour symmetries on CP-odd operators of the Warsaw basis. Asymmetries of simple triple products are proven to be more sensitive than the cross-section to these SMEFT operators and more efficient than most of the usual observables used in experiments. Then, on-shell effects of singlet and octet scalars of SUC(3) are independently searched for in four-top production. The signal consists of the associated production of a scalar with a top quark pair and of pair production in the case of the octet producing boosted top-quark pairs. The processes are generated at next-to-leading order in quantum chromodynamics with a careful renormalisation procedure and the correct selection of loop diagrams. Using improvements in top-jet tagging techniques and in the main background simulation as well as a new technique to select the events, a new particle search is performed to constrain the respective mass and coupling to top quarks of new resonances.

</details>


### [113] [Central exclusive production of $η$ and $η'$ mesons in diffractive proton-proton collisions at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39503)
*Piotr Lebiedowicz,Otto Nachtmann,Antoni Szczurek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用张量pomeron方法研究了高能质子-质子碰撞中η和η'(958)介子的中心快度区间双衍射产生过程，并结合振幅层次的吸收效应，利用WA102实验数据约束模型参数，预测了LHC 13 TeV能量下的截面与微分分布，表明该过程可用于在LHC上探测pomeron的本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究中心快度区间双衍射产生的η和η'(958)介子，以理解pomeron的本质，特别是其在味单态赝标量介子产生中的作用。

Method: 采用张量pomeron模型，将吸收效应纳入散射振幅中，并用WA102实验（√s = 29.1 GeV）数据标定模型参数，进而外推至LHC能量（√s = 13 TeV）进行理论预测。

Result: 预测了pp→ppη和pp→ppη'在LHC能量下的总截面：η为2.5 μb（|η_M|<1）和5.6 μb（2<η_M<5）；η'为0.3–0.7 μb和0.9–2.1 μb，分别对应相同快度区间。

Conclusion: 中心快度区间双衍射产生η和η'介子的过程在LHC上具有可观测性，是研究pomeron性质、尤其是其张量结构与味单态耦合的理想探针。

Abstract: We discuss central exclusive production (CEP) of $η$ and $η'(958)$ mesons in high-energy proton-proton collisions using the tensor-pomeron approach, incorporating absorption effects at the amplitude level. Model parameters are constrained using WA102 experimental data measured at $\sqrt{s} = 29.1$ GeV. We present cross sections and differential distributions for the LHC energy of $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV. For $pp \to pp η$, the upper limit of the total cross section is 2.5 $μ$b for $|η_{M}| < 1$ and 5.6 $μ$b for $2 < η_{M} < 5$. For $pp \to pp η'$, the predicted cross sections are 0.3--0.7 $μ$b and 0.9--2.1 $μ$b in the respective pseudorapidity regions. These results demonstrate the feasibility of studying diffractive flavour singlet pseudoscalar meson production to probe the nature of the pomeron at the LHC.

</details>


### [114] [Exclusive production of $π^+ π^-$ pairs in diffractive $γp$ and in $pp$ collisions within the tensor-pomeron approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39520)
*Piotr Lebiedowicz,Otto Nachtmann,Antoni Szczurek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在张量pomeron框架下研究了高能γp和pp碰撞中π⁺π⁻对的排它性产生，涵盖ρ(770)、ω、f₂(1270)共振及Drell-Söding非共振机制；成功拟合H1实验数据，并预测了LHC 13 TeV下pp→ppπ⁺π⁻微分截面，结果适用于LHC各探测器及未来电子-质子/离子对撞机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能强子与光子碰撞中π⁺π⁻对的排它性产生机制，特别是共振线形受Drell-Söding过程的影响，以及为LHC和未来对撞机实验提供理论基准。

Method: 采用张量pomeron模型，同时纳入ρ(770)、ω、f₂(1270)共振态与Drell-Söding非共振贡献，分析γp→π⁺π⁻p和pp→ppπ⁺π⁻过程，并与H1数据对比，外推至LHC能量。

Result: 模型在M_ππ ≲ 1.2 GeV范围内良好描述H1数据；揭示Drell-Söding机制对共振线形的关键调制作用；给出√s=13 TeV下pp→ppπ⁺π⁻的微分截面预测，适用于快速度间隙选样下的LHC实验及超外围pA/AA碰撞。

Conclusion: 张量pomeron框架下共振与非共振机制协同主导π⁺π⁻排它性产生；该理论可统一解释当前γp数据并可靠外推至LHC及未来电子-离子对撞机相关过程。

Abstract: We discuss exclusive production of $π^+ π^-$ pairs in diffractive $γp$ and in $pp$ collisions at high energies, considering resonant ($ρ(770)$, $ω$, $f_2(1270)$) and non-resonant (Drell-Söding) contributions within the tensor-pomeron approach. For the $γp \to π^+ π^- p$ reaction, the model describes well the H1 data in the region $M_{ππ} \lesssim 1.2$ GeV. We discuss the important role of the Drell-Söding mechanism in shaping the resonance line. We also predict differential cross sections for the $pp \to pp π^+ π^-$ reaction at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV where at least one proton emits a virtual photon. These findings are relevant for the central exclusive production of $π^+ π^-$ pairs in the context of ALICE, ATLAS, CMS, and LHCb measurements in hadron-hadron collisions at the LHC, especially under experimental selections using only rapidity-gap conditions. Additionally, our results are also applicable to $π^+ π^-$ production in ultraperipheral $p$A/AA collisions at the LHC and to photo- and electroproduction of $π^+ π^-$ at future electron-proton/ion colliders (EIC, LHeC).

</details>


### [115] [Supermassive charged gravitinos and the Lux-Zeplin event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39529)
*Krzysztof A. Meissner,Hermann Nicolai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种替代WIMP解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验中单个异常事件的新假说：超重分数电荷引力微子作为暗物质候选者，并预测其会产生微秒级内连续核反冲信号以区别于WIMP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN实验观测到一个异常事件，被初步解释为WIMP信号；作者旨在提供一种基于超重分数电荷引力微子的替代理论解释，并解决WIMP与该新模型在截面和通量上的本质差异问题。

Method: 基于前期提出的超重分数电荷引力微子暗物质模型，重新估算核反冲能谱与事例率，并分析其在探测器中产生多步直线轨迹反冲的特征信号；同时提出实验升级方案以区分WIMP与引力微子假说。

Result: 250 keV核反冲能量及一年内单事件的观测与引力微子模型估算一致；关键可检验预言是微秒级内沿直线轨迹发生两个或更多连续反冲，该信号可明确区分于WIMP并突破中微子地板限制。

Conclusion: 超重分数电荷引力微子是一种可行的暗物质候选者，其独特信号特征为未来直接探测实验提供了新的判据和升级方向。

Abstract: The Lux-Zeplin collaboration recently reported one unusual event which was interpreted as first evidence for a WIMP (arXiv:2609.02823[hep-ex]). In this note we offer an alternative explanation of this event, following our earlier proposal that dark matter could consist of fractionally charged supermassive gravitinos. A main difference between the two interpretations is that one expects small cross sections and large flux rates for WIMPs, whereas it is the opposite for supermassive gravitinos, namely large (electromagnetic) cross sections and very low flux rates. The observed nuclear recoil energy of about 250 keV and the occurrence of one event in one year fit well with the estimates quoted in (K.A. Meissner, H.~Nicolai, Eur.Phys.J.C 84 (2024) 3, 269). A main characteristic of the new interpretation distinguishing it unequivocally from the WIMP hypothesis would be the sequential occurrence of two or more recoils within a few microseconds along the gravitino's straight trajectory, a prediction that remains to be checked. Such a measurement would also allow to overcome the `neutrino floor'. In this note we refine our previous estimates and propose some extra steps so as to enable the experiment to discriminate between the WIMP and gravitino hypotheses.

</details>


### [116] [Exact color sums for multi-gluon amplitudes: direct, multiplet and symmetric-group Fourier methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39574)
*Rikkert Frederix,Valentin Hirschi,Malin Sjödahl*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种计算全胶子树级平方矩阵元的方法：直接迹和伴随分解收缩、正交多重态基底法、以及基于对称群不可约表示的快速傅里叶变换（FFT）法；结果表明，FFT方法在6–11个胶子情况下最快，尽管其阶乘复杂度高于多重态递归的指数复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高多胶子过程色求和矩阵元的计算效率，应对高阶微扰计算中组合爆炸带来的挑战。

Method: 对比三种方法：(1) 直接迹与伴随分解收缩；(2) 基于SU(3)表示的正交多重态基底与振幅递归；(3) 利用对称群不可约表示、作用于胶子标签排列的FFT方法，结合色序分振幅。

Result: 在11胶子情形下，直接伴随收缩耗时约1700秒，多重态递归为19.8秒，而伴随FFT仅需0.814秒；FFT在6–11胶子范围内始终最快。

Conclusion: 尽管FFT具有阶乘时间复杂度，但在实际胶子数（6–11）范围内显著优于其他方法，因其最大限度利用了胶子排列对称性；该方法为高阶QCD计算提供了高效新途径。

Abstract: We compare three approaches for computing color-summed tree-level all-gluon squared matrix elements: evaluation using direct contraction in the trace and adjoint decompositions, orthonormal multiplet bases, and fast Fourier transforms (FFT) based on the irreducible representations of the symmetric group for the trace and adjoint decompositions. The multiplet method reduces the color sum to a sum of absolute squares and utilizes an amplitude recursion labeled by SU(3) representations. The FFT exploits the relative-permutation dependence of color overlaps to replace the direct double sum with smaller independent contractions, using standard color-ordered partial amplitudes. This invokes the symmetric group at the level of gluon labels, and therefore makes maximal use of the permutation symmetry. We compare CPU time and memory use for four through eleven gluons. At eleven gluons, the direct adjoint contraction evaluates a fixed-helicity color-summed matrix element in an estimated 1.7 10${}^3$ s after initialization, compared with 19.8 s for the multiplet recursion and 0.814 s for the FFT adjoint method. The adjoint FFT is fastest from six through eleven gluons, despite its factorial scaling compared with the exponential scaling of the multiplet recursion.

</details>


### [117] [On the Role of Diffractive Production in Precision Studies of W and Z Bosons at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39582)
*Ynyr Harris,Matthias Schott,Chen Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了单衍射W和Z玻色子产生对大型强子对撞机（LHC）上电弱玻色子精确测量的影响，通过结合DYTurbo、Herwig和Pythia8等工具，评估了不同能量下对强耦合常数α_s和W玻色子质量m_W的系统偏移，结果表明其影响在当前精度下不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LHC测量精度提高，需全面理解电弱玻色子产生的所有贡献，尤其是单衍射过程对精度观测量的潜在影响。

Method: 采用DYTurbo计算非衍射基准截面，Herwig提供单衍射相对贡献及其横动量依赖，Pythia8动态多部分子相互作用模型引入p_T依赖的快度间隙存活概率；通过确定性逐箱重加权构建衍射Asimov谱，并对非微扰参数进行轮廓分析。

Result: 在5、7、8、13 TeV下，α_s的标称相对偏移分别为-0.0072%、-0.043%、-0.070%、-0.067%；五种Pythia8 Pomeron通量与衍射PDF配置导致Δα_s最大离散为9.4×10⁻⁶；W质量分析中未建模单衍射贡献最大偏差为1.58 MeV，模板校正后剩余偏差为1.14 MeV。

Conclusion: 所考察模型下，单衍射贡献对m_W的物理显著影响可忽略，其引起的系统偏移低于当前拟合精度。

Abstract: The increasing precision of measurements at the Large Hadron Collider requires a detailed understanding of all contributions to electroweak boson production. We estimate the impact of single-diffractive W and Z production on precision observables at $\sqrt{s}=5$, 7, 8, and 13 TeV. The non-diffractive baseline is calculated with DYTurbo, while Herwig provides the relative single-diffractive contribution and its transverse-momentum dependence. Factorization breaking is incorporated through a $p_T$-dependent rapidity-gap survival probability calculated with the dynamic multiparton-interaction model of Pythia8. The diffractive Asimov spectrum is constructed by deterministic bin-by-bin reweighting of the same high-numerical-precision DYTurbo cross section used for the non-diffractive baseline. With the non-perturbative parameters profiled, the nominal relative shifts in $α_s$ are -0.0072%, -0.043%, -0.070%, and -0.067% at 5, 7, 8, and 13 TeV, respectively. Five Pythia8 Pomeron-flux and diffractive-PDF configurations give a maximum spread of $9.4\times10^{-6}$ in $Δα_s$. In the W-mass study, the largest bias from an unmodelled survived single-diffractive contribution is 1.58 MeV, and the largest residual after applying the corresponding model-matched template correction is 1.14 MeV. These shifts are below the relevant fit precision, and no phenomenologically significant impact on $m_W$ is expected within the tested models.

</details>


### [118] [The holographic QCD critical point is sensitive to quark flavors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39598)
*Niko Jokela,Matti Järvinen,Toshali Mitra,Aleksi Piispa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用味依赖的全息V-QCD模型研究致密QCD物质的相结构，发现其相图对模型拟合方法和物理环境（如beta平衡或重离子碰撞条件）高度敏感；在beta平衡下存在低密度临界端点的一阶相变，但在重离子碰撞条件下该相变消失，这可能解释RHIC束流能量扫描中尚未观测到明显非单调涨落信号的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释RHIC束流能量扫描中净质子高阶累积量未显示清晰非单调涨落信号的原因，并厘清致密QCD物质相结构对物理环境和模型参数的依赖性。

Method: 采用味依赖的全息V-QCD模型，结合格点QCD热力学拟合，分别分析电荷中性、beta平衡物质与固定电荷-重子数比（n_Q/n_B = 0.4）的奇异数中性物质；计算化学冻结曲线上的重子数高阶累积量比，并与Thermal-FIST包中的强子共振气体模型进行匹配比较。

Result: beta平衡下存在一阶相变及其临界端点，但位置显著低于早期全息研究结果；重离子碰撞条件下该相变完全消失；高阶重子数累积量比预测与实验数据可定量比较；V-QCD与强子共振气体模型匹配所得全局相图进一步支持上述结论。

Conclusion: 致密QCD物质的相结构具有强烈环境依赖性，这为理解重离子碰撞实验中缺乏明确临界信号提供了新视角，也凸显了在理论建模中准确刻画物理约束条件的重要性。

Abstract: We study the phase structure of dense QCD matter by applying the flavor-dependent holographic V-QCD model to two distinct physical environments: charge-neutral, beta-equilibrated matter, and strangeness-neutral matter with a fixed charge-to-baryon number ratio, n_Q/n_B = 0.4, relevant for heavy-ion collisions. The quark sector of the model incorporates the realistic mass hierarchy of two massless light quarks and a massive strange quark. We find that the resulting phase diagram depends sensitively on both the procedure used to tune the model against lattice QCD thermodynamics and the choice of physical environment. Using our preferred fitting procedure, we find that beta-equilibrated matter exhibits a first-order phase transition terminating at a critical endpoint located at significantly lower densities than in earlier holographic studies. However, this phase transition disappears entirely under heavy-ion conditions. This pronounced environmental dependence may help explain why recent net-proton cumulant measurements from Phase II of the RHIC Beam Energy Scan have not yet revealed clear non-monotonic fluctuation signatures. We calculate higher-order baryon number cumulant ratios along the chemical freeze-out curve to quantitatively compare the model predictions with experimental data. Finally, we also present global phase diagrams obtained by matching V-QCD to Hadron Resonance Gas models implemented in the Thermal-FIST package, and argue that this comparison further supports the conclusions drawn from the V-QCD model alone.

</details>


### [119] [Diffractive Vector-Meson Production from the Light-Front Quark Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39647)
*Satyajit Puhan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在色偶极子图像下，利用两种自旋-轨道光前波函数（S-1和S-2）研究ρ⁰、φ、J/ψ和ψ(2S)介子的排他性衍射电生过程；S-1源自光前夸克模型中的Melosh-Wigner旋转，S-2为自旋改进形式；二者共享相同径向波函数，参数由LFQM哈密顿量确定；CGC偶极参数由包含结构函数数据固定，未拟合衍射数据；S-1更优描述轻介子观测量，S-2更优描述粲偶素比值及J/ψ衰变常数；还给出了四种介子的电荷、磁和四极矩形状因子并与格点QCD结果比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重味介子（如J/ψ, ψ(2S)）与轻味介子（如ρ⁰, φ）在排他性衍射电生中的不同行为，并检验不同自旋-轨道波函数对介子结构和产生机制的描述能力。

Method: 采用色偶极子图像，结合两种自旋-轨道光前波函数（S-1和S-2），其中S-1基于Melosh-Wigner旋转，S-2为自旋改进形式；所有波函数使用相同径向部分，参数由光前夸克模型哈密顿量确定；色玻璃凝聚（CGC）偶极参数由包容性结构函数数据标定；未对衍射数据进行额外拟合。

Result: S-1波函数更优再现轻介子（ρ⁰, φ）的截面、R=σ_L/σ_T、t和W依赖性及σ_φ/σ_ρ比值；S-2波函数更优描述粲偶素比σ_{ψ(2S)}/σ_{J/ψ}和J/ψ衰变常数；同时计算并对比了四种介子的电荷、磁和四极矩形状因子与格点QCD结果。

Conclusion: 不同自旋-轨道波函数对轻味与重味介子的衍射产生具有互补描述能力，表明介子内部自旋-轨道耦合结构对过程动力学有显著影响；该工作为区分介子内部波函数模型及进一步约束QCD非微扰结构提供了理论依据与实验可检验预言。

Abstract: We study exclusive diffractive electroproduction of the $ρ^0$, $φ$, $J/ψ$, and $ψ(2S)$ mesons in the color-dipole picture using two spin-orbit light-front wave functions, S-1 and S-2. S-1 follows from the Melosh--Wigner rotation in the light-front quark model (LFQM), while S-2 is the spin-improved form commonly used in holographic and boosted-Gaussian approaches. Both share the same radial wave function, with their parameters determined from the LFQM Hamiltonian. The color-glass-condensate dipole parameters are fixed from inclusive structure-function data, with no fit to diffractive data. We compare the predictions with measurements of cross sections, $R=σ_L/σ_T$, $t$ and $W$ dependences, and $σ_φ/σ_ρ$. S-1 gives a better description of the light-meson observables, while S-2 provides a better description of the charmonium ratio $σ_{ψ(2S)}/σ_{J/ψ}$ and the $J/ψ$ decay constant. We also present the charge, magnetic, and quadrupole form factors of the four mesons and compare them with available lattice-QCD results.

</details>


### [120] [Hadronic light-by-light scattering in AdS/QCD and the muon $g-2$: tensor meson contributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39720)
*Jonas Mager,Luigi Cappiello,Josef Leutgeb,Anton Rebhan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了全息QCD在硬墙AdS/QCD模型中对赝标量和轴矢量介子对轻强子光-光散射振幅（进而对缪子反常磁矩）贡献的预测，并探讨了张量介子在满足短程约束中的作用，提出了一个与BELLE及BESIII实验数据吻合良好的最小张量介子模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个纯强子模型，以满足Melnikov-Vainshtein等短距约束，并解释缪子反常磁矩中的轻强子光-光散射贡献。

Method: 基于硬墙AdS/QCD全息模型，分析赝标量、轴矢量及张量介子对轻强子光-光散射振幅的贡献；推导短距极限下的解析结果，并与OPE（算符乘积展开）下的零质量夸克圈结果对比。

Result: 该模型能自然满足Melnikov-Vainshtein约束；所提出的最小张量介子模型与BELLE及BESIII低Q²数据高度一致；短距极限解析结果与OPE结果相符。

Conclusion: 全息QCD框架下，引入适当张量介子自由度可构建自洽、数据兼容的纯强子轻强子光-光散射模型，为缪子g−2理论计算提供可靠强子输入。

Abstract: We briefly review the predictions of holographic QCD in hard-wall AdS/QCD models for the contribution of pseudoscalars and axial-vector mesons to the hadronic light-by-light amplitude and consequently to the muon anomalous magnetic moment, which allows one to satisfy the Melnikov-Vainshtein constraint in a purely hadronic model. Moreover, we discuss the role of tensor mesons in satisfying the remaining short-distance constraints, and provide details for a minimal model of tensor mesons that turns out to agree remarkably well with available data from BELLE as well as preliminary low-$Q^2$ data from BESIII. In an appendix, we also provide analytical results for the short-distance limits in hQCD and compare with the leading-order OPE result as given by the massless quark loop.

</details>


### [121] [Enhancing Constraints on Ultralight Axion Dark Matter from Gravitational Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39775)
*Pierce Giffin,Pankaj Munbodh,Elisa G. M. Ferreira*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超轻轴子在太阳引力场中的捕获效应，发现其可形成太阳束缚的引力原子态，显著增强局部暗物质密度，并据此更新了轴子-光子耦合强度的实验限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻轴子作为暗物质候选者，其在天体引力场中的行为及对局部密度的影响尚需深入研究，以改进相关实验探测灵敏度。

Method: 理论建模与计算：分析超轻轴子在太阳引力势中的玻色增强捕获过程，计算其形成的引力原子态及对地球处局部轴子暗物质密度的增强效应，并结合现有和未来实验数据推导轴子-光子耦合上限。

Result: 对于轴子质量在10^{-14}–10^{-13} eV范围、衰变常数fa ~ 3.5×10^7 GeV的情形，地球处轴子暗物质密度可超过银河系标准值ρ₀ = 0.4 GeV/cm³的十倍；据此更新了卫星与地面实验对轴子-光子耦合gaγ的限制，并给出未来实验的更优预期灵敏度。

Conclusion: 太阳对超轻轴子的引力捕获可导致显著的局部密度增强，该效应必须纳入轴子暗物质搜寻的理论解释与实验分析中，从而提升探测能力与物理约束精度。

Abstract: Ultralight axions can be gravitationally captured by massive bodies such as the Sun, producing solar-bound gravitational atom states that amplify the local dark matter density through Bose-enhanced capture. For axion masses in the range $10^{-14}\text{eV}\lesssim m_a \lesssim 10^{-13}\text{eV}$, this mechanism becomes exponentially efficient. We show that, for a representative decay constant $f_a\sim 3.5\times10^7$ GeV, the local axion dark matter density at Earth can grow to more than ten times the standard Galactic value of $ρ_0 = 0.4 \text{ GeV/cm}^{3}$. Incorporating this overdensity, we derive updated limits on the axion-photon coupling from existing satellite and terrestrial measurements and present improved projections for upcoming experiments.

</details>


### [122] [Gravitational waves in the complex-singlet-extended Manohar-Wise model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39781)
*Benhong Mei,Yabo Dong,Jun Fan,Jingya Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩展Manohar-Wise模型中宇宙相变与随机引力波信号，重点分析Z3对称性破缺导致的畴壁及其湮灭，并评估其在LISA、TianQin、BBO及SKA等引力波探测器频段内的可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索离散对称性破缺（Z3）引发的宇宙相变与畴壁动力学，及其产生的可探测引力波信号，以补充当前粒子物理与宇宙学观测手段。

Method: 构建含复标量单态的Manohar-Wise扩展模型；分析单步与两步热演化历史下的相变特性；结合理论与实验约束，估算声波、磁流体湍流和畴壁湮灭三类引力波源的谱形与强度。

Result: 所选基准点的相变引力波信号主峰位于10^-3–10^-2 Hz，覆盖LISA等空间干涉仪灵敏度范围；当畴壁能量偏差调至使畴壁信号峰在~2×10^-9 Hz时，两步相变模型信号可达SKA脉冲星计时阵列灵敏度，而单步模型信号低于当前纳赫兹探测水平。

Conclusion: 毫赫兹与纳赫兹频段引力波观测具有互补性，可协同检验该模型中的相变机制与离散对称性破缺。

Abstract: We investigate cosmological phase transitions and stochastic gravitational-wave signals in the Manohar-Wise model extended by a complex scalar singlet. Spontaneous breaking of a discrete Z3 symmetry produces domain walls, whose subsequent annihilation is driven by a small explicit symmetry-breaking bias. Subject to theoretical and experimental constraints, we study two thermal histories: a one-step strong first-order electroweak phase transition satisfying the baryon-number preservation criterion, and a two-step history whose first transition is a first-order transition along the singlet direction, leaving electroweak symmetry unbroken. We estimate the gravitational-wave contributions from sound waves, magnetohydrodynamic turbulence, and domain-wall annihilation. Within the adopted approximations, the phase-transition signals of the selected benchmarks peak at approximately 10^-3 -10^-2 Hz and overlap the projected sensitivity regions of space-based observatories such as LISA, TianQin, and BBO. Choosing the vacuum-energy bias to fix the domain-wall peak at approximately 2x10^-9 Hz, we find that the larger domain-wall tensions of the two-step benchmarks yield signals reaching the projected SKA sensitivity, whereas the one-step sample remains below the pulsar-timing sensitivities considered. These results illustrate the complementarity of millihertz and nanohertz gravitational-wave observations in probing phase transitions and discrete-symmetry breaking in this model.

</details>


### [123] [Axial Symmetry Breaking and Chiral Separation Effect in Nambu-Jona-Lasinio Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39886)
*Xin-Nan Zhu,Xin-Li Sheng,Shu-Yun Yang,Shijun Mao,Defu Hou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在三味NJL模型中研究了手性分离效应（CSE）和轴向伙伴关联函数劈裂，对比了反磁催化（IMC）与磁催化（MC）两种耦合方案下轴向动力学在热密磁场物质中的行为，发现CSE电导率和轴向对称性恢复特征对磁场、温度和化学势敏感，并与格点QCD结果定性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用物质中轴向对称性（特别是U(1)_A）在高温高密及外加磁场下的恢复行为，及其与手性分离效应等新型输运现象的联系。

Method: 基于三味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (NJL) 模型，分别采用场依赖标量耦合（导致反磁催化IMC）和常数耦合（导致磁催化MC），计算弱磁场极限下的手性分离电导率及轴向伙伴介子关联函数劈裂Δ_A和Δχ，并分析其随温度、化学势和磁场的变化。

Result: CSE电导率在低温被压制、高温趋近手性极限值，且IMC与MC下磁场依赖性相反；Δ_A和Δχ在高温/高密下被压制，反映轴向对称性恢复；二者之差在相变附近出现尖锐峰，表明U(1)_A^{(0)}与U(1)_A^{(3)}对称性在不同温/密处恢复；IMC方案下零化学势结果与格点QCD定性一致，并给出了有限密度下的预测。

Conclusion: 轴向动力学在磁化热密物质中表现出丰富结构，CSE与轴向关联劈裂可作为探测U(1)_A对称性部分恢复的有效探针，IMC框架更符合当前格点QCD证据。

Abstract: We investigate the chiral separation effect (CSE) and the axial-partner susceptibility splittings within the three-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model, providing two complementary aspects of axial dynamics in hot and dense magnetized matter. We compare a field-dependent scalar coupling, which leads to inverse magnetic catalysis (IMC), with a constant coupling, which exhibits magnetic catalysis (MC). In the weak-field limit, the CSE conductivity is suppressed at low temperature and approaches its chiral-limit value as the temperature increases. Its magnetic-field dependence is reversed between the IMC and MC scenarios. On the other hand, the susceptibility splittings $Δ_A = χ_{π^0} - χ_{δ^0}$ and $Δχ= χ_{π^0} -χ_{σ_l}$ are suppressed at high temperature or density, reflecting the substantial restoration of axial symmetries. Their difference develops a pronounced peak near the cross-over or first-order transition, implying that the $U(1)_A^{(0)}$ and $U(1)_A^{(3)}$ symmetries are restored at different temperatures or chemical potentials. Both the weak-field CSE and the IMC susceptibility splittings at vanishing chemical potential agree qualitatively with lattice-QCD calculations. We also provide model predictions for the susceptibility splittings at finite density.

</details>


### [124] [Schur elimination of brane-localised Higgs mass spectra, and the criteria for the $ε\to0$/$N\to\infty$ non-commutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39910)
*Roberto Barceló,Subhadip Mitra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在5维标准模型扩展中，当希格斯玻色子局域在额外维度某一点并与体费米子耦合时，费米子质量谱计算中因边界调节器处理顺序不同（先取极限还是先求和KK塔）导致结果不一致的问题，并指出该不一致性本质上源于一个条件收敛级数的不连续性，且该现象具有普适性，可推广至普通散射、光学定理、跷跷板机制、门户机制和钟表机构等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在5维扩展标准模型中，由于希格斯局域化导致的费米子质量谱计算中因极限与求和顺序不同而产生歧义的问题。

Method: 利用Schur补精确消去整个Kaluza-Klein塔，将歧义归结为单个级数的不连续性，并直接证明其存在所需两个普适条件。

Result: 发现该歧义源于条件收敛级数的不连续性，其出现需满足两个普适条件：耦合足够尖锐以使级数条件收敛，且存在另一通道在接触点处呈现互补行为；这两个条件不依赖于额外维度或离散KK谱的存在。

Conclusion: 该歧义问题具有广泛适用性，可用于判断接触相互作用、光学定理虚部、跷跷板、门户及钟表机制等各类模型中是否会出现类似极限顺序依赖的不确定性。

Abstract: In many 5D extensions of the Standard Model, the Higgs boson is localised to a single point along the extra dimension, where it couples to bulk fermions. Computing the fermion mass spectrum then requires a boundary regulator, and removing it before or after summing the Kaluza-Klein tower can give different answers, as shown in Ref.~\cite{Barcelo:2014kha}. Because the coupling reaches any KK mode through only two overlap numbers, the whole tower can be eliminated exactly by a Schur complement; the disagreement between the two orders of limit then reduces to a single sum, whose discontinuity we prove directly. This discontinuity needs two conditions to appear: a coupling sharp enough to leave the sum conditionally convergent, and a second channel with complementary behaviour at the point of contact. Neither condition depends on having an extra dimension, or even a discrete tower to sum. The same test applies to contact interactions in ordinary scattering, the imaginary part behind the optical theorem, and seesaw, portal, and clockwork constructions. Whether the two conditions are met determines whether a similar ambiguity plays any role.

</details>


### [125] [Electromagnetic probes of concurrent minijet-hydrodynamics evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39939)
*Soham Banerjee,Mayank Singh,Charles Gale,Sangyong Jeon,Daniel Pablos,Jean-François Paquet*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合minijet与流体力学的并发框架，用于研究直接光子和双轻子在重离子碰撞中的产生，发现minijet对等离子体时空演化的影响可被电磁探针显著探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁探针（如直接光子和双轻子）在整个QGP演化过程中持续发射，能直接反映其时空历史，而强子观测量易被流体参数调整所掩盖，因此需建立能同时描述minijet与介质动力学相互作用的自洽框架。

Method: 构建了事件到事件初始涨落与动力学minijet-介质相互作用相耦合的并发minijet-流体力学框架；使用PYTHIA生成minijets，IP-Glasma提供初态，MUSIC进行流体演化，UrQMD处理强子输运。

Result: minijet通过能量动量沉积改变流体演化，对光子和双轻子谱及各向异性产生可观测影响；该效应无法被单纯调节剪切粘滞或初态归一化所吸收，证实电磁辐射是探测硬部分子与QGP相互作用的灵敏探针。

Conclusion: 电磁探针提供了对minijet与夸克胶子等离子体动力学耦合的独特观测窗口，支持将直接光子和双轻子作为QGP微观输运性质的关键诊断工具。

Abstract: We investigate direct photon and dilepton production in a concurrent minijet-hydrodynamics framework that consistently incorporates event-by-event initial-state fluctuations and dynamical minijet-medium interactions. Produced in initial hard scatterings, minijets thermalize more slowly than the bulk medium and persist into the hydrodynamic stage, where they deposit energy and momentum into the evolving plasma, generating wakes that modify its spacetime evolution. Minijets modify the hadronic multiplicity and momentum anisotropy in a manner that can largely be absorbed by adjusting the shear viscosity and the normalization of the system's initial energy-momentum tensor. In contrast, electromagnetic probes are emitted throughout the evolution and remain directly sensitive to the underlying spacetime history. We perform a comprehensive calculation of direct photon and dilepton production with the minijets and bulk medium evolved simultaneously, using PYTHIA to produce minijets, and describing the underlying plasma with IP-Glasma initial conditions, hydrodynamic evolution with MUSIC and hadronic transport with UrQMD. We find that the minijet-induced modification of the hydrodynamic evolution produces measurable effects in both photon and dilepton observables, establishing electromagnetic radiation as a sensitive probe of the interplay between hard partons and the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [126] [PINNing the pion: conformal deep learning for $F_π(s)$ and the $(g-2)_μ$ hadronic contribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40008)
*Mayank Goel,Subhadip Mitra,Monalisa Patra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINN）的方法，在共形z平面中直接从第一性原理构建π介子电磁形状因子F_π(s)，融合时空域数据、S矩阵基本原理与多种物理约束，实现模型无关的高精度提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统现象学拟合模型存在模型依赖性、非物理伪影和运动学不一致性问题，亟需一种能严格遵循基本物理原理的数据驱动方法。

Method: 构建嵌入共形z平面的物理信息神经网络（PINN），通过损失函数硬编码电荷归一化、Schwarz反射、Cauchy-Riemann解析性、色散关系、Watson定理及pQCD渐近行为；将割复平面映射到单位圆盘以稳定优化；联合e⁺e⁻散射与τ衰变数据，原生分离同位旋矢量分量。

Result: 获得模型无关的π介子电荷半径⟨r_π²⟩ = 0.435 ± 0.008_stat ± 0.007_cali fm²、ρ(770)次级-sheet极点参数（m_ρ^pole = 761.72±1.04 MeV, Γ_ρ^pole = 135.99±1.20 MeV）以及μ子反常磁矩的双π贡献a_μ^{ππ} = (506.48 ± 2.02_stat ± 1.70_cali)×10⁻¹⁰。

Conclusion: 该PINN框架首次在统一、自洽、模型无关的前提下，将S矩阵基本原理与实验数据深度融合，显著提升强子形状因子提取的可靠性，并为检验ρ峰附近实验张力提供了新范式。

Abstract: Extracting the pion electromagnetic form factor $F_π(s)$ through phenomenological curve-fitting models introduces model dependence, unphysical artefacts, and kinematic inconsistencies. We introduce a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) embedded in a conformal $z$-plane that constructs $F_π(s)$ directly from first principles across spacelike and timelike domains: charge normalisation and Schwarz reflection are enforced by construction, while Cauchy-Riemann analyticity, dispersion relations, Watson's theorem, and perturbative QCD asymptotics enter through the loss functional. Thus, the fundamental S-matrix principles dictate the form factor's behaviour while data act as constraints. Mapping the cut complex plane onto the unit disk bounds the Hessian norm and prevents Neural Tangent Kernel spectral starvation, two known failure modes of deep-learning optimisation. Besides $e^+e^-$ scattering data, we also incorporate $τ$-decay data through a switch that isolates the pure isovector form factor natively, bypassing model-dependent isospin-breaking pre-corrections. The network organically yields an interior zero-free form factor, while the framework tests experimental tensions around the $ρ(770)$ peak against analyticity and dispersion constraints. We obtain model-independent estimates of the pion charge radius, $\langle r_π^2 \rangle = 0.435 \pm 0.008_{\text{stat}} \pm 0.007_{\text{cali}}$ fm$^2$, the second-sheet pole parameters, $m_ρ^{\text{pole}} = 761.72\pm 1.04$ MeV and $Γ_ρ^{\text{pole}} = 135.99 \pm 1.20$ MeV, and the two-pion contribution to the muon anomalous magnetic moment, $a_μ^{ππ} = (506.48 \pm 2.02_{\text{stat}} \pm 1.70_{\text{cali}}) \times 10^{-10}$.

</details>


### [127] [Production Shape Effects Beyond Rate Rescaling in Dark Photon Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40052)
*X. Zhong,H. Y. Qiu,X. Q. Shen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了长寿命粒子搜索中产生模型不确定性对实验接受度的影响，特别关注动力学分布而非总产额。通过比较修正的Weizsäcker-Williams描述与有效准实近似（QRA）在暗光子质子轫致辐射产生中的差异，并在共同物理区域内归一化谱，发现NA62实验中接受度差异显著（δ_NF > 0.3），表明产率因子化不成立；同时公开的NA62全选择接受度在混合与轫致辐射两类产生机制间呈现非恒定比值，进一步证实接受度依赖于产生机制。文章提出“投影判据”作为检验因子化的定量方法，并强调当该判据不满足时，必须将产生模型依赖性纳入接受度计算或作为系统误差处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命粒子搜索中，产生模型不确定性不仅影响总产额，还可能影响被实验探测到的运动学分布；现有分析常隐含‘产率因子化’假设（即接受度与产生谱可分离），但该假设缺乏严格验证，可能导致系统偏差。

Method: 1）采用修正Weizsäcker-Williams模型与有效准实近似（QRA）建模暗光子在质子轫致辐射中的产生；2）在共同物理定义域内归一化二者微分产额，以隔离运动学形状差异对接受度的影响；3）计算NA62束流倾倒几何下的接受度差异δ_NF；4）分析HEPData中公开的NA62全选择接受度在Mixing与Bremsstrahlung两类产生机制下的dimuon接受度比值；5）提出‘投影判据’：要求归一化产生形状变化在接受度响应上的投影可忽略。

Result: 1）在NA62灵敏度重叠区域所有收敛网格点上，δ_NF > 0.3，且该结果对QRA离壳尺度（1.0–2.0 GeV）及孔径变化鲁棒；2）公开全选择接受度中，Mixing与Bremsstrahlung的dimuon接受度比值在0.58–4.23间变化，中位绝对偏离为~0.79，证实接受度依赖于产生类别；3）‘投影判据’可定量检验因子化是否成立。

Conclusion: 产率因子化在NA62等实验中不成立，产生模型对接受度有不可忽略的动力学依赖；因此，在精度要求较高时，必须将产生模型依赖性显式传播进接受度计算，或将其列为接受度系统误差；‘投影判据’为此类评估提供了通用、定量的框架。

Abstract: Production model uncertainties in long-lived particle searches can affect both the inclusive yield and the kinematic distributions sampled by the experimental acceptance. We isolate the latter effect by comparing the modified Weizsäcker--Williams description with the effective quasireal approximation for dark photon production via proton bremsstrahlung after normalizing both spectra on a common physical domain. At fixed production yield within this domain, the resulting acceptance difference directly measures the departure from rate factorization. In the NA62 beam dump geometry, the central calculation gives a fractional acceptance shift $δ_{\rm NF}>0.3$ at every numerically converged grid point within the displayed NA62 sensitivity overlap. This conclusion persists for effective QRA off-shell scales of 1.0, 1.5, and 2.0~GeV and under the aperture variations considered. Separately, the public NA62 full selection acceptances give a nonconstant dimuon acceptance ratio between the Mixing and Bremsstrahlung production categories, ranging from approximately 0.58 to 4.23, with a median absolute departure from unity of approximately 0.79 over the analyzed HEPData grid. This provides complementary evidence that the published full selection acceptance depends on the production category. More generally, the projection criterion provides a quantitative test of rate factorization. The normalized production shape variation must have negligible projection onto the acceptance response at the required accuracy. When this condition is not satisfied, production model dependence must be propagated through the acceptance calculation or treated as an acceptance uncertainty.

</details>


### [128] [New Physics in $ b \to s τ^+τ^-$ Processes and Correlations with $R(D^{(*)})$ and $B\to K^{(*)}ν\barν$: An (SM)EFT Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40072)
*Guillermo Baltà,Andreas Crivellin,Joaquim Matias,Martín Novoa-Brunet*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了b→sτ⁺τ⁻过程，作为探测与第三代夸克和轻子相关的新物理的敏感探针，并在弱有效理论和SMEFT框架下分析了新物理对B→K^{(*)}τ⁺τ⁻、B_s→φτ⁺τ⁻等衰变的影响，预测其分支比可能增强数个数量级，未来实验将能检验相关参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受R(D^{(*)})异常及B^+→K^+νν̄增强迹象的启发，本文旨在更新并深入研究对新物理敏感的tau轻子味改变中性流过程b→sτ⁺τ⁻。

Method: 基于先前包含ψ(2S)共振的数据驱动方法，给出B→K^{(*)}τ⁺τ⁻和B_s→φτ⁺τ⁻在全运动学范围内的预测；在弱有效理论中给出分支比的半解析表达式；并在维度-6 SMEFT框架下系统分析TeV尺度新物理。

Result: 识别出当前味物理数据偏好的SMEFT参数空间区域，其中b→sτ⁺τ⁻过程的分支比可增强若干数量级；指出不同新物理场景虽对B→K^{(*)}νν̄预言相同，却可通过B→K^{(*)}τ⁺τ⁻、B_s→τ⁺τ⁻和B_s→φτ⁺τ⁻测量加以区分。

Conclusion: 尽管当前实验灵敏度仍远高于标准模型预言，但由现有味物理反常所暗示的增强将使LHCb、CMS和Belle II的未来结果能有效检验相关SMEFT参数空间。

Abstract: Tauonic flavour-changing neutral-current transitions ($b\to sτ^+τ^-$) are sensitive probes of New Physics connected to third-generation quarks and leptons. Motivated by the persistent anomalies in $R(D^{(*)})$ and the indications of enhanced $B^+\to K^{+}ν\barν$ rates, we revisit and update the predictions for $b\to sτ^+τ^-$ processes. Building upon a previous work on a data-driven approach to include the dominant $ψ(2S)$ resonances, we provide predictions for $B\to K^{(*)}τ^+τ^-$ ($B_s\toφτ^+τ^-$) over the full kinematic range. To include possible New Physics contributions within the Weak Effective Theory, we provide semi-analytic expressions for the corresponding branching ratios. We then perform a comprehensive dimension-6 SMEFT analysis of New Physics realised at the TeV scale. We identify regions of parameter space preferred by current data where the branching ratios of $b\to sτ^+τ^-$ processes can be enhanced by several orders of magnitude. While current experimental sensitivities remain far above the SM rates, the enhancements suggested by present flavour data imply that forthcoming limits from LHCb, CMS, and Belle~II will probe significant regions of the relevant SMEFT parameter space. Furthermore, we highlight that while different scenarios can lead to the same predictions for $B\to K^{(*)}ν\barν$ branching ratios, they can be disentangled by measuring $B\to K^{(*)}τ^+τ^-$, $B_s\toτ^+τ^-$ and $B_s\toφτ^+τ^-$.

</details>


### [129] [Estimating High-Energy Neutrino Effective Areas from Muon Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40188)
*Stephan A. Meighen-Berger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解析计算缪子在中微子望远镜中传播距离及能量损失统计特性的新方法，精度高、参数少，可广泛用于灵敏度分析、能量重建和跨探测器一致性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子望远镜依赖缪子探测中微子，而缪子在进入探测器前经历长距离随机能量损失，传统蒙特卡洛模拟计算成本高，亟需高效、可解析、可迁移的物理建模方法。

Method: 建立缪子传播与能量损失的解析模型，仅用两个探测器相关参数即可刻画其穿越介质后的可探测距离及能量衰减统计分布，并与蒙特卡洛模拟及实测有效面积对比验证。

Result: 该模型对蒙特卡洛传播结果的复现误差≤3%，对IceCube、KM3NeT/ARCA和P-ONE有效面积的拟合误差约1%；并成功应用于点源灵敏度估计、能量重建与多探测器一致性检验。

Conclusion: 该解析模型有效解耦了缪子输运物理与探测器响应，显著提升建模效率与可移植性，为新型探测器或能量损失模型提供快速基准测试手段。

Abstract: Neutrino telescopes observe neutrinos through the muons produced by charged-current interactions. Most of those muons are born kilometers outside the detector, and how much energy they lose on the way is stochastic. We show that we can compute analytically the distance a muon travels before it falls below the detection threshold, while describing the statistical properties of its losses. Our result reproduces Monte Carlo propagation to within $3\%$, and with two parameters per detector it matches the effective areas published by IceCube, KM3NeT/ARCA, and P-ONE to about $1\%$. We then show how this model can be used in point-source sensitivities, energy reconstruction, and consistency checks between detectors. The calculation disentangles muon-transport physics from a detector's response, so a new detector or loss model can be quickly benchmarked.

</details>


### [130] [In medium evolution of the energy-energy correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40224)
*Weiyao Ke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次从第一性原理出发，分析了夸克和胶子喷注在QCD物质中传播时双点能量-能量关联函数（EEC）随能标的演化，并基于软共线有效理论（SCET）结合Glauber相互作用，建立了介质中EEC的因子化框架；在一阶微扰和领对数精度下计算了介质修正的喷注函数，发现存在一个实验可及的动力学区域，其中介质效应直接体现为反常维数的修正，从而为探测介质内动力学提供了清晰探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已在初态碰撞中成功应用的能量-能量关联（EEC）拓展至核-核或质子-核等含介质反应中，以实现对QCD介质内喷注淬火机制的精确、微扰可算的理论描述。

Method: 基于软共线有效理论（SCET）并引入Glauber胶子相互作用构建介质中EEC的因子化公式；采用不透明度展开方法，在一环水平计算介质修正的夸克与胶子喷注函数，并进行领对数（LL）大对数项重求和。

Result: 获得介质中EEC的解析表达式，识别出一个实验上可观测的运动学区域，其中介质效应直接表现为喷注函数反常维数的修正；理论预测与p-Pb碰撞中EEC测量结果一致，并可推广至O-O小系统及电子-离子对撞机（EIC）。

Conclusion: EEC在强子-核碰撞中可作为高精度探针，其介质修正具有清晰的微扰可算性与物理图像，为定量理解喷注与QCD介质相互作用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: In this work, we present a first-principles analysis of the scale evolution of the two-point energy-energy correlator (EEC) for quark and gluon jets propagating through QCD matter. The EEC is a jet substructure observable that encodes the angular distribution of energy flow within jets and has proven valuable for precision tests of QCD in elementary collisions. Extending this framework to reactions with nuclei, we derive a factorized description of the in-medium EEC using Soft Collinear Effective Theory with Glauber gluon interactions, allowing systematic inclusion of medium-induced interactions. Working in the opacity expansion, we compute the medium-modified quark and gluon jet functions at one loop and perform leading-logarithmic resummation of large-scale logarithms. We find an experimentally accessible kinematic regime where medium effects manifest directly through medium-induced corrections to the anomalous dimensions, providing a transparent probe of in-medium dynamics. We test the theoretical framework by comparing our analytic predictions with EEC measurements in p-Pb collisions and discuss its implications for small collision systems (O-O) and the EIC.

</details>


### [131] [$\mathcal{O}(αα_s^2)$ corrections to the Top Quark mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40229)
*Daniele Gaggero,Ciaran Williams*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了三圈混合QCD-EW修正对顶夸克质量的影响，建立了极点质量和MS-bar质量之间的关系，并发现该修正量级与当前实验误差相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定顶夸克质量是粒子物理标准模型检验的关键，而高阶QCD与电弱混合修正对质量定义转换至关重要，尤其在当前实验精度下需评估其影响。

Method: 通过将三圈自能图表示为三圈主积分（MIs），并构造ε-因子化形式的微分方程求解这些MIs；涉及椭圆曲线周期等特殊数学结构。

Result: 得到了O(αα_s²)阶下极点质量Mt与MS-bar质量mt(μ)的转换关系；对Mt=172.3 GeV，mt(Mt)的修正为−216 MeV。

Conclusion: 三圈混合QCD-EW修正不可忽略，其大小（−216 MeV）已达当前实验不确定度量级，必须纳入理论预测以匹配实验精度。

Abstract: We calculate the three-loop $\mathcal{O}(αα_s^2)$ mixed QCD-EW corrections to the top quark mass and obtain the relationships between the pole ($M_t$) and $\overline{\rm{MS}}$ ($m_t(μ)$) masses at this order. In performing our calculation, we express the three-loop self-energies as a function of three-loop master integrals (MIs). These MIs are defined so as to solve a differential equation in $ε$-factorized form, allowing for their solution to be expressed as iterated integrals. We discuss the types of differential forms that we encounter, which include periods of elliptic curves, and their role in the final physical result. Using our results, we calculate the shift in $m_t(M_t)$ arising at this order. For $M_t = 172.3$ GeV, the shift is -216 MeV, which is comparable to the currently quoted experimental uncertainty.

</details>


### [132] [Interpreting the High-Recoil LUX-ZEPLIN Event with Bino-/Singlino-like and Higgsino Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40234)
*Subhojit Roy,Pedro Schwaller,Carlos E. M. Wagner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验观测到的约248 keV核反冲事例在超对称理论中的两种解释：弹性散射（基于中性微子与Z玻色子的轴向耦合）和非弹性散射（伪狄拉克希格斯ino），分别对应不同中性微子成分与质量劈裂机制，并分析其与暗物质丰度、Higgs质量及当前实验限制的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验新发现的248 keV核反冲候选事件，并检验其在超对称框架（MSSM/NMSSM）下作为暗物质信号的可行性，同时协调宇宙学丰度、电弱精度与现有探测极限。

Method: 构建两类超对称中性微子模型——弹性散射模型（依赖Higgsino不对称参数X的Z玻色子轴向耦合）与非弹性散射模型（伪狄拉克希格斯ino的百keV量级质量劈裂）；结合LZ灵敏度、热退耦丰度计算、Higgs质量约束及太阳中微子限制进行参数扫描与基准点分析。

Result: 弹性解释支持NMSSM中~500 GeV singlino-bino混合中性微子，X≈0.007，通过近阈值类单态赝标量共振实现正确剩余丰度并压低当前湮灭；非弹性解释需PeV级gaugino或singlino混合产生~few hundred keV劈裂，但受太阳中微子实验强约束；两类模型在偏离248 keV处具有可区分的反冲谱。

Conclusion: LZ候选事件可在超对称框架下自洽解释，但弹性通道更易满足多重要求（尤其在NMSSM中），而非弹性通道面临更强天体物理约束；未来低能反冲谱测量将有效区分这两种机制。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has recently reported a nuclear-recoil candidate at $E_R\simeq248$ keV. We study two supersymmetric interpretations based on distinct neutralino couplings to the $Z$ boson. In the elastic case, a bino- or singlino-like neutralino scatters through a diagonal axial $Z$ coupling set by the Higgsino asymmetry $X=|N_{13}|^2-|N_{14}|^2$ rather than by the total Higgsino fraction. Since the spin-dependent blind spot does not coincide with the spin-independent one, Higgs-mediated scattering can be suppressed while retaining $X\sim10^{-2}$. In the MSSM this favors low $\tanβ$, in tension with the observed Higgs mass for a few-TeV squark spectrum, a tension relieved by the tree-level quartic of the NMSSM. An explicit NMSSM benchmark with $m_χ\simeq500$ GeV and $X\simeq0.007$, below current LZ limits, obtains its relic abundance from a singlet-like pseudoscalar resonance just above threshold, which also suppresses present-day annihilation. In the inelastic case, a pseudo-Dirac Higgsino scatters endothermically, with a splitting of a few hundred keV generated by gauginos at the PeV scale which can be lowered by opposite-sign bino-wino cancellation, or by weak singlino mixing; the minimal thermal realization is strongly constrained by solar-neutrino searches. The two mechanisms predict distinct recoil spectra away from the candidate.

</details>
