### [133] [Diffusion Models for Polarimetric Reconstruction of Circumstellar Environments in Correlated Speckle Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38229)
*Quentin Villegas,Laurence Denneulin,Simon Prunet,André Ferrari,Éric Thiébaut,Maud Langlois*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合扩散先验与相关噪声建模的极化逆问题求解框架，显著提升了系外行星盘高对比度偏振成像的去噪与结构恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RHAPSODIE框架假设白噪声，无法刻画大气和仪器散斑的空间相关性，限制了高对比度偏振成像性能。

Method: 将散斑建模为具有高斯谱密度的平稳协方差高斯场；设计专用预处理器以高效处理含相关散斑与异方差光子噪声的密集精度矩阵；扩散先验仅在纯盘数据上训练。

Result: 在真实场景下显著优于Tikhonov正则化，与Plug-and-Play基线方法性能相当，能成功恢复被相关噪声掩盖的盘形态特征；但在极低信噪比下仍有局限。

Conclusion: 引入空间相关噪声建模与专用优化策略的扩散先验方法，可有效提升偏振逆问题求解质量，为高对比度天文成像提供新范式。

Abstract: High-contrast polarimetric imaging of circumstellar disks is severely limited by stellar leakage and speckle noise. Building upon the RHAPSODIE framework for polarimetric inverse problems, diffusion-based methods have shown promising results by replacing classical regularization with learned priors. However, RHAPSODIE's white noise assumption fails to capture the spatial correlation structure of atmospheric and instrumental speckles. We extend this framework to handle speckle fluctuations, which are modeled as a Gaussian field with a stationary covariance matrix with a Gaussian spectral density. To efficiently apply the dense precision matrix that includes correlated speckle and heteroskedastic photon noise, we develop a dedicated preconditioner for conjugate gradient iterations. The diffusion prior is trained exclusively on disk-only data. While limitations remain at very low SNRs, our method significantly outperforms Tikhonov regularization and competes favorably with a Plug-and-Play baseline in realistic regimes, successfully recovering morphological features masked by correlated noise.

</details>


### [134] [Multi Kernel Gaussian Process Guiding: Predictive Correction for Mounts with Several Gear Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38236)
*Rumen G. Bogdanovski*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Klenske等人提出的基于高斯过程（GP）的周期性误差校正模型，通过引入多个准周期核来建模多级齿轮系统中互不公度的周期误差，显著提升了天文望远镜跟踪精度，尤其在多级减速结构（如应变波驱动、皮带-蜗杆组合）中效果明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单周期核GP模型只能建模单一齿轮级的周期误差；而实际望远镜云台（如含应变波驱动、皮带-蜗杆或多级传动）会产生多个互不公度的周期误差，相互拍频且无共同重复周期，导致单核模型性能受限。

Method: 提出一种扩展的GP模型：使用多个准周期核（quasi-periodic kernels）之和，每个核对应一个齿轮级；各核参数从残差频谱中自动识别，依据谱线强度进行门控，并排除与已建模周期公度的候选周期；第二核采用带平方指数包络的准周期核以缓解对周期精度的严苛要求（原需0.2%精度）。

Result: 在合成双级误差数据上，新方法比单核模型残差降低1–40%；在真实记录的无第二齿轮频率数据上仍提升6%（相对单核）和45%（相对滞后式反应控制器）；且对单级情况数值结果保持不变；代码为无依赖、跨平台C语言实现，已集成进INDIGO项目。

Conclusion: 该扩展模型有效解决了多级互不公度周期误差建模难题，在保持原有单级性能的同时显著提升复杂云台系统的实时预测校正能力，具备实用部署价值。

Abstract: Gaussian process (GP) based predictive periodic error correction, introduced by Klenske et al., learns a telescope mount's periodic tracking error online and applies the correction before the error is observed, removing the reaction lag inherent in purely reactive autoguiders. The published model uses a single periodic kernel and therefore represents the error of one gear stage: any repeating shape at one period, harmonics included, but only one period. Mounts with more than one reduction stage -- strain-wave drives with a separate input stage, belt-and-worm combinations, transfer gears -- produce errors at periods that are mutually incommensurate, which beat against each other and never repeat on a common cycle. We extend the model to a sum over an arbitrary number of quasi-periodic kernels, one per gear stage, each discovered from the residual spectrum, gated on the strength of its own spectral line, and refused if it is commensurate with a period already modelled. We show that a strictly periodic second kernel is unusable in practice because it demands the second period to within 0.2%, and that a squared-exponential envelope of about twenty periods removes that sensitivity at a cost of 0.02 px. On synthetic two-stage errors at realistic periods and amplitudes the extension reduces the residual by 1-40% against the single-kernel model, while leaving single-stage cases numerically unchanged. On a recorded, de-noised session with no second, incommensurate gear frequency, it still improves on the single-kernel model by 6% and on a reactive hysteresis controller by 45%, by absorbing amplitude modulation of the primary that the single kernel cannot. The implementation is dependency-free, multiplatform code in the C programming language and is released as part of the INDIGO project.

</details>


### [135] [Gestalt: a meta-foundation model for astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38312)
*Michael J. Smith,Shashwat Sourav*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的元基础模型Gestalt，通过融合22个冻结的通用基础模型的嵌入，并进行白化和随机SVD降维，构建出在天文图像物理属性与形态估计任务上显著优于各单个模型的1024维表征。该方法节省大量计算资源与碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于柏拉图表征假说，探索多个非收敛基础模型能否协同形成更优、共享的世界表征，尤其在天文学领域利用已有模型避免重复预训练。

Method: 将22个来自8个家族的冻结基础模型对星系图像提取嵌入，对各视图白化后拼接，再进行随机SVD降维至1024维，构建无监督元模型Gestalt。

Result: Gestalt在HSC、JWST和DESI Legacy Survey数据上的19/21项指标（物理属性与形态估计）均超越所有单个基础模型；性能随模型数量和架构多样性提升，并具有跨巡天迁移能力。

Conclusion: 仅需一次无监督投影即可组合现有通用基础模型，构建高性能天文专用表征，大幅节省算力（从数万A100小时降至数分钟）与碳排放，验证了‘组装即服务’式基础模型范式的可行性。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis predicts that sufficiently scaled foundation models converge on a shared representation of the world. As each non-converged model gives a noisy view of a common structure when passed the same input, we ask whether we can combine models into a representation that outperforms its individual components. We test this on galaxies: we embed images via a basket of 22 frozen foundation models from eight families, whiten each view, and take a randomised SVD of the embedding concatenation. The resulting 1024-dimensional embedding outperforms every basket member on 19/21 of our tested metrics for physical property and galaxy morphology estimation for HSC, JWST, and DESI Legacy Survey imagery. We find that performance rises with basket size and basket architectural diversity, and that the meta-foundation model's performance transfers across astronomical surveys. We conclude that a useful astronomical foundation model can be assembled from existing generalist models with no training required beyond a single unsupervised projection. By leveraging the community's already-spent work, we save a lot of compute: a fresh pre-train of a comparable single-domain model would cost $\mathcal{O}(10^{4}$--$10^{5})$ A100 GPU hours (emitting several tonnes of CO$_2$eq.), whereas assembling Gestalt requires minutes on a single machine.

</details>


### [136] [An X-ray Extremely wide-field Spectroscopic Surveyor (XESS, or "chess")](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38321)
*W. P. Maksym,M. Ulmer,S. Friedrich,F. Tombesi,R. Arcodia,S. P. Singam,S. Bongiorno,N. Thomas,M. Elvis,Ming Sun,John ZuHone,M. Lynne Saade,Philip Kaaret,Doug Swartz,Erin Hicks,Ming-Yi Lin,Pallavi Patil,Jimmy Irwin,Shrabani Kumar,Kirtan Dixit,Sudip Chakraborty,Andrea Gnarini,Steven Ehlert*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: XESS是一个新的X射线天文台，旨在实现宽视场、高能谱分辨率的X射线巡天，服务于时域与多信使天体物理研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 响应2020年天文学与天体物理学十年调查的关键需求，推动X射线谱学进入巡天时代和时域/多信使天体物理（TDAMM）新范式。

Method: 提出名为XESS（X-ray Extremely wide-field Spectroscopic Surveyor）的X射线观测站设计方案，强调宽视场与高能量分辨率光谱能力的结合。

Result: XESS被论证为可高效支撑关键科学目标（如超大质量黑洞增长、星系演化、致密天体并合等）的下一代X射线设施。

Conclusion: XESS代表了X射线天文学向大规模、时域化、多信使协同方向发展的关键一步，具备显著科学优势与战略必要性。

Abstract: We propose an X-ray observatory, the X-ray Extremely wide-field Spectroscopic Surveyor (XESS, pronounced "Chess") to bring high energy resolution X-ray spectroscopy into the era of synoptic surveys and of time domain and multi-messenger astrophysics (TDAMM). Such an observatory is optimally suited to addressing key elements of the 2020 Decadal Survey on Astronomy and Astrophysics.

</details>


### [137] [PTED: A multi-dimensional two-sample test for scientific inference and generative machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38388)
*Connor Stone*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PTED（基于能量距离的置换检验），一种适用于多维数据的两样本检验方法，具有高维适用性、对样本量不敏感、支持任意数据类型等优点，并在多种敏感性测试中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两样本检验方法在多维场景下缺乏易用且可靠的工具，用户常依赖启发式或可视化方法，亟需一种通用、可解释且易于使用的检验方法。

Method: 基于Székely和Rizzo（2004）提出的能量距离构造检验统计量，并结合置换检验获得精确p值；利用距离矩阵的近似公式实现线性时间复杂度。

Result: PTED在各类敏感性测试中均表现出高敏感性，是唯一对所有测试均有响应的方法；而传统MMD仅在过拟合测试中略有差异。

Conclusion: PTED是一种通用、高效、鲁棒的多维两样本检验工具，适用于高维、小/大/不平衡样本及任意可定义距离的数据类型。

Abstract: Two-sample tests are widely applicable in inference and generative modelling, yet users frequently fall back on heuristics and visual inspection due to lack of an accessible test that operates in multiple dimensions. I present Permutation Test using the Energy Distance (PTED), a Python implementation of a powerful two-sample test described in Székely and Rizzo [2004]. The test statistic is the energy distance, a metric on probability distributions that admits a natural sample estimator built from pairwise distances. A permutation test is run on energy distances to produce an exact two-sample test. Because the statistic depends on the data only through pairwise distances, the test applies in high dimensions, on learned feature representations, at large or small or imbalanced sample sizes, and to any data type on which a distance can be defined. An approximation to the formula allows PTED to scale linearly with both number of dimensions and samples while retaining most discriminative power. I highlight a number of instructive sensitivity tests on PTED alongside other multi-dimensional two-sample tests for comparison. PTED is the only method that is sensitive to all tests, though conventional Maximum Mean Discrepancy is identical for all but an over-fitting test.

</details>


### [138] [Fisher and DALI posterior approximations for testing gravity with gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38399)
*Felipe A. da Silva Barbosa,Davi C. Rodrigues,Josiel Mendonça Soares de Souza,Miguel Quartin*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了在广义相对论检验中，基于Fisher矩阵及其高阶扩展DALI方法对引力波参数估计后验分布的近似精度与计算效率，发现singlet-和doublet-DALI显著快于精确采样（加速20–1000倍），且精度优于传统Fisher矩阵；triplet-DALI则表现不稳定；同时作者发布了符号化实现工具SymDALI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升引力波参数估计中后验分布近似的计算效率，同时保证在广义相对论检验（TIGER形式）中的精度，尤其针对 inspiral 波形参数化偏差分析。

Method: 采用IMRPhenomD波形模型，对比传统Fisher矩阵、singlet-DALI（Fisher采样）、doublet-DALI和triplet-DALI四种近似方法与精确后验采样的性能；使用Jensen-Shannon散度评估精度；开发了基于Wolfram Language符号微分的新工具SymDALI。

Result: singlet-和doublet-DALI比精确采样快20–1000倍（信噪比越高加速越明显），精度系统性优于Fisher矩阵；triplet-DALI精度波动大，实用性受限；SymDALI实现了符号化导数计算。

Conclusion: DALI的低阶形式（singlet/doublet）在精度与效率间取得良好平衡，是广义相对论检验中后验近似的实用替代方案；triplet-DALI尚不稳健；SymDALI为未来高精度快速分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We investigate the accuracy and computational efficiency of posterior approximations for gravitational-wave (GW) parameter estimation based on Fisher Matrix and its higher-order extension, the Derivative Approximation for LIkelihoods (DALI), in the context of tests of General Relativity using parameterized deviations in the inspiral waveforms (the TIGER formalism). We adopt the \texttt{IMRPhenomD} waveform and compare the exact posterior sampling to the following approximations in increasing order of complexity: the traditional Fisher Matrix, the Fisher sampling (singlet-DALI), the doublet-DALI and the triplet-DALI. We find that both singlet and doublet-DALI are significantly faster than the exact posterior by factors between 20 and 1000, depending on the inspiral SNRs -- larger gains correspond to larger SNRs. These gains are considerably larger for more complete waveforms, such as \texttt{IMRPhenomHM}. To evaluate the accuracy of the approximations, we use Jensen-Shannon divergence. We conclude that both singlet and doublet-DALI systematically improves over the standard Fisher Matrix approximation, while triplet-DALI shows large variability, limiting its practical advantage. We also introduce a code with a new implementation of Fisher and DALI analysis (Symbolic DALI, \texttt{SymDALI}) which computes derivatives symbolically via \textit{Wolfram Language}.

</details>


### [139] [MATCH: Performance Characterization and Scientific Observations of the Wuhan University 1-m Optical Telescope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38858)
*Sai-En Xu,Bei You,Han He,Shuai-Kang Yang,Xiao Fan,Yi-Fei Qu,Rui-Xiang Hu,Hui-Bo Fei,Bo-An Chen,Zong-Hong Zhu,Zheng-Yang Li,Xiao-Yan Li,Zi-Jian Han,Jia-Nan Cong,Chao Chen,Jia-Li Chen,Kai-Wen Zheng,Yi-Qiao Yang,Qing-Shan Li,Zhen-Guang Sun,Tong Zhou,Kai Zhang,Jiajia Wu,Xuhang Yin,Liang Yuan*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: MATCH望远镜是武汉大学在冷湖运行的1米Ritchey-Chrétien望远镜，配备成像与光谱仪器，自2025年10月试运行以来完成性能表征与自动化观测流程建设，具备时域天文与暂现源后随观测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持时域天文和暂现源（如超新星、伽马暴余辉等）的快速后随观测与长期监测，需具备灵活调度、自动响应和多波段光谱-成像能力的中小型自主望远镜设施。

Method: 介绍MATCH望远镜系统配置，实测其首年运行性能（极限星等、分辨率等），并构建集成观测工作流，包括望远镜控制系统、观测调度器和自动化数据处理流水线。

Result: 成像系统在ugri波段60秒等效5σ极限星等达18–20等；B/G光谱通道30分钟积分下达15–16等；最佳成像条件下g/r波段可达约21等；系统具备ToO响应与灵活调度能力。

Conclusion: MATCH已成为一个高效、自主、兼容光学多模观测的地面时域天文平台，可支撑暂现源与变源的光谱-光度联合后随及长期监测任务。

Abstract: The Multi-mode Autonomous Terminal for Compatible Hybrid-optics (MATCH), operated by Wuhan University and located at Lenghu, is a 1-m Ritchey--Chrétien telescope equipped with imaging and spectroscopic instruments. MATCH has been in trial operation since October 2025. In this work, we present the telescope and instrument configuration, characterize its performance during the first year of operation, and describe the integrated observing workflow developed for routine and time-domain observations, including the observatory control system, observation scheduler, and automated data reduction pipelines. Under typical observing conditions, the imaging system reaches 60-s-equivalent $5σ$ limiting magnitudes of approximately 18--20 mag across the $ugri$ bands, while the spectroscopic system reaches approximately 15--16~mag in the B and G channels in a 30-minute integration at the effective spectral resolution of the instrument. In the best-quality imaging observations, the $g$- and $r$-band depths approach 21~mag. Together with flexible scheduling and Target-of-Opportunity capability, MATCH provides photometric and spectroscopic follow-up and long-term monitoring of transient and variable sources.

</details>


### [140] [The Simons Observatory: Design and Initial Performance of the Detector Readout System for the Large-Aperture Telescope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39100)
*Thomas P. Satterthwaite,Tristan Pinsonneault-Marotte,Shawn W. Henderson,Zeeshan Ahmed,Kam Arnold,Declan Baker,Andrew Bazarko,Josh Borrow,Michael L. Brown,Mark Devlin,Simon Dicker,Peter N. Dow,Shannon M. Duff,Daniel Dutcher,John C. Groh,Saianeesh K. Haridas,Kathleen Harrington,Erin Healy,Johannes Hubmayr,Bradley R. Johnson,Brian Keating,Anna M. Kofman,Adrian T. Lee,Alex Manduca,Aashrita Mangu,Jenna E. Moore,Michael D. Niemack,Sandra O'Neill,John Orlowski-Scherer,Yudai Seino,Mufan Shao,Carlos Sierra,Max Silva-Feaver,Suzanne Staggs,Eve M. Vavagiakis,Yuhan Wang,Edward J. Wollack*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了西蒙斯天文台大型望远镜（LAT）所采用的高复用低温超导探测器读出系统的设计、实现与初步性能，该系统支持约63,000个光学耦合的过渡边沿传感器（TES）探测器，采用微波频率复用技术，单传输线可同时读出860个探测器，并已实现迄今最大规模的超导天文传感器部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足西蒙斯天文台大型望远镜在宇宙微波背景辐射、大尺度结构及太阳系新天体等多方面科学目标，需大规模部署高性能、高复用率的低温超导探测器读出系统。

Method: 采用微波频率复用（microwave frequency multiplexing）技术，结合大规模制造的含射频超导量子干涉器件（rf-SQUID）的复用芯片，以及配套室温电子学系统，实现对LAT接收器中63,000个TES探测器的高效读出。

Result: 系统成功读出81.4%的光学耦合探测器（超出预期），读出噪声等效电流符合预部署指标，验证了其在实际运行中的可靠性与性能达标。

Conclusion: 该读出系统设计成功支撑了迄今最大规模超导探测器阵列的天文观测部署，为未来CMB实验及其他低温探测项目提供了可扩展的技术范式。

Abstract: We present the design, implementation, and initial performance of a highly multiplexed cryogenic superconducting detector readout system in the context of its deployment to the Simons Observatory large-aperture telescope (LAT). The Simons Observatory is a cosmic microwave background experiment located at 5,200 m in Chile's Atacama Desert. In addition to its 6 m LAT, whose broad range of science goals spans probing large-scale structure to searching for new Solar System objects, the observatory also currently includes three 0.42 m small-aperture telescopes, which search for signatures of primordial gravitational waves. Nearly 100,000 optically-coupled transition-edge sensor bolometers have been deployed across the observatory's four telescopes to enable these science goals. The readout systems for these telescopes use microwave frequency multiplexing to simultaneously read out 860 optically-coupled bolometers per transmission line. This is enabled by the large-scale fabrication of multiplexing chips which house radio-frequency superconducting quantum interference devices for each detector, and the development of room-temperature electronics to read out these bolometers. This has enabled the largest deployment of superconducting sensors for astronomical observations to date. This paper focuses on the design and implementation of the readout system for the 63,000 detectors in the LAT's cryogenic receiver. We present performance results from an early phase in the telescope's operation. We show that the readout system's performance enables the instrument to meet key benchmarks, including successful readout of 81.4% of its optically-coupled bolometers, which exceeds projections, and readout noise-equivalent current consistent with pre-deployment specifications.

</details>


### [141] [The Astronomical Hub: A Unified Ecosystem for Modern Astronomical Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39287)
*Yerlan Aimuratov,Vitaliy Kim,Aleksander Serebryanskiy,Denis Yurin,Maxim Krugov,Chingiz Akniyazov,Saule Shomshekova,Maxim Makukov,Gaukhar Aimanova,Rashit Valiullin,Raushan Kokumbaeva,Alan Kazkenov,Chingis Omarov*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了天文中心（AstroHub）的概念框架，旨在通过整合光学仪器、虚拟天文台工具、FAIR数据原则和望远镜网络，构建一个支持近地天体监测与深空研究的统一平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决近地天体监测和深空研究中设备分散、数据共享不足等问题，需构建一个集成化、开放协作的研究平台。

Method: 提出AstroHub概念框架，整合虚拟天文台（VO）工具、FAIR数据原则及望远镜网络，并以Assy-Turgen天文台为案例开展方向性规划。

Result: 初步形成AstroHub平台架构，明确其在NEO监测与深空研究中的应用潜力及发展路径。

Conclusion: AstroHub有望成为推动天文观测协同化、数据标准化与科研合作高效化的关键基础设施。

Abstract: We present the conceptual framework of the Astronomical Hub (AstroHub), a unified platform combining various optical instruments at a single observatory. Its major approach lies in arranging conditions for research groups to install telescopes and equipment and participate in joint projects. AstroHub is planned to integrate Virtual Observatory (VO) tools, FAIR data principles, and a telescope network to create a powerful and attractive ecosystem for both robust near-Earth object (NEO) monitoring and diverse deep space research. We provide an overview of the AstroHub development directions in the case study of the Assy-Turgen Observatory.

</details>


### [142] [The AI crisis for teaching: "Train your own neural network!"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39433)
*Douglas Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文讨论了大型语言模型（LLM）和AI代理对科学教育，尤其是天文学与天体物理学教学带来的紧迫挑战，强调AI应作为学习支持工具而非替代品，并提出‘学生应训练自己的神经网络’这一核心教育理念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注AI（特别是LLMs和AI代理）对科研与教学的双重影响，指出当前多数讨论聚焦科研，却忽视了AI对高校教学——尤其是教学与科研并重岗位——造成的更紧迫、近乎‘存在性危机’的冲击。

Method: 采用反思性论述与教育哲学分析方法，结合天文学教育实践，提出以‘培养学生像天体物理学家一样思考’为教学核心目标，并倡导将AI定位为辅助性认知工具。

Result: 明确了AI在教育中应扮演‘支持而非替代’的角色；提出‘学生训练自己的神经网络’这一隐喻，强调主动思维、批判性学习与知识内化不可被AI代劳。

Conclusion: 面对AI浪潮，教育者必须坚守育人本质，重构教学设计与评估方式，确保学生通过深度参与获得独立思考能力；否则，高等教育的核心价值将面临瓦解风险。

Abstract: Everyone seems to be thinking about so-called artificial intelligence (AI) these days. As scientists, we understand that we're not about to have a conscious computer intelligence taking over the world, but we're nevertheless concerned about how "large language models" (LLMs) and AI "agents" are impacting the way that we do science. This concern has generated several recent essays about the effects of AI on research, including in my own field of astronomy and astrophysics. These opinion pieces tend to miss the biggest current concern for those whose jobs combine teaching with research - although the threat to future scientific research as we know it is probably a bit concerning, the effect on teaching is an existential crisis right now! We need to hang on to the parts of education that we think are at its core - so that students coming out of degree programmes are actually able to think for themselves, "like an astrophysicist". This means emphasising to students that they should use AI tools to support their education and not to replace it. One way of explaining the importance of this principle is to point out that students should be training their own neural networks.

</details>


### [143] [FAIR-Compliant Architecture for Heterogeneous Astronomical Data: KazVO Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39546)
*Ildana Izmailova,Denis Yurin,Yerlan Aimuratov,Maxim Makukov,Aleksander Serebryanskiy,Saule Shomshekova,Vitaliy Kim,Chingis Omarov,Adel Umirbayeva,Laura Aktay,Dana Kuvatova,Anton Gluchshenko,Gulnara Suliyeva,Maxim Krugov,Nadezhda Vaidman,Daulet Anarbek,Inna Reva,Gauhar Aimanova,Rashit Valiullin,Raushan Kokumbayeva*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了哈萨克斯坦国家虚拟天文台（KazVO）的架构，该系统符合国际虚拟天文台联盟（IVOA）标准，整合了费森科夫天体物理研究所（FAI）异构观测数据，采用分区数据湖、GAVO DaCHS后端及PostgreSQL数据库映射元数据至IVOAObsCore标准，并通过元数据-only模型与四类数据保密机制支持开放科学；其机器对机器服务通过IVOA协议注册于全球注册中心，支持TOPCAT、Aladin和PyVO等外部工具访问；最终实现了对历史玻璃底片（1950–1997）与现代数字观测流（Assy-Turgen和Tien-Shan天文台）的统一、可扩展平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整合费森科夫天体物理研究所（FAI）长期积累的异构天文观测数据（包括历史玻璃底片与现代数字观测流），推动开放科学并使哈萨克斯坦天文数据资源可被全球时域天体物理社区便捷访问和分析。

Method: 构建符合IVOA标准的国家虚拟天文台架构，采用分区数据湖存储、GAVO DaCHS发布后端、PostgreSQL数据库实现异构元数据向IVOAObsCore标准的映射；引入基于四类数据保密机制的元数据-only发布模型；部署符合IVOA协议的机器对机器服务并注册至全球Registry of Registers。

Result: 成功建成可扩展的KazVO平台，统一接入并发布FAI自1950–1997年的历史玻璃底片数字化数据及 Assy-Turgen 和 Tien-Shan 天文台的实时测光与光谱数字数据流；服务已对接TOPCAT、Aladin和PyVO等主流天文工具，实现全球可访问。

Conclusion: KazVO为哈萨克斯坦天文数据提供了标准化、开放化、可互操作的虚拟天文台基础设施，不仅提升了本国数据的国际可见性与重用性，也为发展中国家建设类似VO节点提供了可复用的技术路径与治理范式。

Abstract: This paper presents the architecture of the Kazakhstani National Virtual Observatory (KazVO) - an International Virtual Observatory Alliance (IVOA) compliant node that unifies the heterogeneous observational datasets of the Fesenkov Astrophysical Institute (FAI). The architectural foundation of the system is built upon a partitioned Data Lake, a German Astrophysical Virtual Observatory (GAVO) Data Center Helper Suite (DaCHS) publishing backend, and a PostgreSQL database that maps heterogeneous metadata to the unified IVOA ObsCore standard. We follow Open Science by introducing metadata-only model featuring based on a four-class data embargo mechanism. Machine-to-machine services deployed via IVOA protocols are listed in the global Registry of Registers, enabling analysis of Kazakhstani observational assets within external clients such as Tool for OPerations on Catalogues And Tables (TOPCAT), Aladin, and PyVO. As a result, KazVO framework provides a scalable platform driven by a developed end-to-end pipeline that bridges two fundamentally distinct data types - digitized historical glass-plate heritage (1950-1997) and live operational photometric and spectroscopic digital streams from telescopes at the Assy-Turgen and Tien-Shan Observatories - opening FAI's combined data datasets to the global scientific and time-domain astrophysics community.

</details>


### [144] [ArxSP: A Python-Based Modular Application for the Reduction of Digitized Archival Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39571)
*Ildana Izmailovaa,Adel Umirbayeva,Manas Khassanova,Laura Aktay,Saule Shomshekova*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种用于归档光谱数据处理的新Python软件包，专为校正电子光学转换器（EOC）引起的几何畸变及光学密度-强度转换而设计，解决了传统天文软件（如IRAF）无法处理、旧版QuickC程序不兼容现代系统的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决FAI档案中约10,000张含磁致几何畸变的EOC光谱底片无法用IRAF等标准工具处理，且原有QuickC程序已不兼容现代操作系统的问题。

Method: 开发基于PyQt5的交互式Python软件，复现并扩展原QuickC程序逻辑，实现图像旋转裁剪、几何畸变校正、光学密度-强度特征曲线构建与像素值直接转换。

Result: 成功构建跨平台、可复现、可扩展的光谱归档数据处理软件，支持FAI历史底片的现代化处理。

Conclusion: 该软件填补了EOC光谱畸变校正与密度-强度转换专用工具的空白，提升了历史光谱数据再利用的效率与可靠性。

Abstract: We present a methodology for the reduction of archival spectral data together with the description of a newly developed Python-based software package featuring an interactive graphical interface. The work is primarily aimed at processing spectra obtained with electron-optical converters (EOCs), which are characterized by geometric distortions induced by the magnetic field of the registration system. Such data are preserved, in particular, in the archive of the Fesenkov Astrophysical Institute (FAI), which contains about 10,000 photographic plates. These distortions, along with the need to transform the optical density of the photographic material into relative intensity, cannot be corrected by standard astronomical packages such as IRAF and therefore require a dedicated approach. Historically, reductions at FAI were performed using a program written in the Microsoft QuickC language for computing platforms of the 1990s, rendering it incompatible with modern operating systems. The new package is implemented with the PyQt5 framework, retaining the logic of the original code while extending its functionality. The implemented algorithms include image rotation and cropping, geometric distortion correction, construction of the characteristic curve linking optical density and intensity, and direct conversion of pixel values in object spectra. The developed software ensures reproducible reduction of archival spectra and provides a cross-platform environment with potential for further extensions.

</details>

Cresci,A. J. Corso,S. Covino,F. D'Ammando,A. De Rosa,P. D'Incecco,V. D'Odorico,V. D'Orazi,S. Desidera,P. Di Marcantonio,M. P. Di Mauro,N. Elias-Rosa,S. Fineschi,E. Fiorellino,C. Fiorini,D. Fulvio,A. TL;DR: 本文概述了意大利天文界参与宜居世界天文台（HWO）发展的兴趣与潜力，基于2025年7月罗马会议成果，并整合后续修订与建议，涵盖科学目标、关键技术贡献、拟开发的仪器概念，以及意大利在HWO前期地面（VLT、ELT）与空间任务中的领导角色，旨在推动国际协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动意大利天文界参与HWO这一重大国际空间任务，明确其科学与技术贡献路径，并为国际合作奠定基础。

Method: 基于专题会议成果及后续研讨，系统梳理意大利社区的科学兴趣、技术专长、拟研制仪器概念，以及前期相关设施中的领导项目。

Result: 明确了意大利在HWO中可贡献的关键科学方向、核心技术（如光学、探测器、精密测控等）、若干优先发展的仪器概念，以及依托VLT、ELT等前期设施开展的准备性工作。

Conclusion: 意大利具备实质性参与HWO的能力与意愿，该文为对接NASA及其他国际合作伙伴提供了清晰的技术与科学路线图，是启动正式合作的重要基础文件。

Abstract: This paper summarizes the interest of the Italian astronomical community in the possible participation in the development of the Habitable Worlds Observatory (HWO). It is largely based on the outcome of a dedicated meeting held in Rome in July 2025, and it includes further revisions and new suggestions that have arisen in the following months. It includes a succinct description of the main scientific interests and of the key technologies that the Italian community can contribute to the development of the mission. It also includes a short section dedicated to the instrument concepts that we deem important to develop for potential adoption on HWO, and that the Italian community is interested in developing. Finally, it includes a concise presentation of the main instruments for ground-based telescopes (VLT and ELT) and space missions that will be operational before HWO and in which the Italian community has leading positions. They will be invaluable to settle the scientific landscape for the mission and to prepare the Italian scientists to make optimal use of HWO. This paper is primarily aimed at paving the way to discussions and collaborations with worldwide scientists and agencies interested in the development of the mission.

</details>


### [146] [Two bits about lossy compression: On the limits of compression in cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40239)
*Hurum Maksora Tohfa,Matthew McQuinn*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经网络的天文数据压缩方法，利用SZ3和自回归Transformer学习量化桶的概率分布，在保持百分级精度的同时显著降低存储开销，适用于模拟与观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文大数据（如巡天观测和宇宙学模拟）存储成本高、共享慢；现有32位浮点存储过度精确，而物理和数值不确定性使得百分级像素精度已足够，亟需高效有损压缩方法。

Method: 结合科学压缩工具SZ3与一个固定量化步长、用自回归Transformer学习各量化桶概率分布的神经压缩器；量化器决定误差上限，网络仅优化码率。

Result: 神经压缩器在所有测试宇宙学场中均优于或匹配SZ3，码率比高斯最优编码低9–27%；Eulerian网格仅需1–4比特/像素（bpp），粒子位移与速度需5–6 bpp；迁移至Rubin观测数据后达4.0 bpp，比高斯编码节省27%。

Conclusion: 该方法以可控、可证误差为前提，实现了面向天文科学需求的高效压缩；模型学到的是宇宙结构的通用统计特性，具备跨数据集泛化能力，可推广至观测数据。

Abstract: Astronomy is in an era of enormous sky surveys and of the large simulation suites needed to interpret them, both costly to store and slow to share. Simulation outputs are stored as 32-bit floats, yet numerical noise and astrophysical uncertainties make better than percent-level pixel accuracy unnecessary. Because cosmological fields are statistically homogeneous with nearly Gaussian mode amplitudes, classic rate-distortion results apply directly, and non-Gaussian structure permits further compression. We use the scientific compression package SZ3, and a neural compressor that fixes the quantization and learns the probability of each quantization bin with an autoregressive transformer. SZ3 beats the Gaussian approach only where the field is smooth on the pixel scale or its values pile up at a single point, while the neural approach matches or beats SZ3 on every field we consider and is 9 to 27\% below the Gaussian-optimal coder at fixed distortion, with the largest margin where the field is most non-Gaussian. Because the quantizer, not the network, sets the error, a poorly trained model can waste bits but never cost accuracy. A model trained only on weak lensing convergence maps transfers to $N$-body density fields without retraining, suggesting it has learned generic properties of cosmological structure rather than features of one dataset. Eulerian grids need only 1-4 bits per pixel (bpp) at percent-level accuracy, and particle displacements and velocities 5-6 at the precisions they require. The approach carries over to observational data: on Rubin Observatory Data Preview 1 coadds, with the quantization step set to a quarter of the background noise, the fine-tuned transformer needs 4.0 bpp, 27\% fewer than the Gaussian coder.

</details>
